# 任务:敌方弹幕绘制尺寸对账(行为 GAP G11)

背景:玩家弹(Arrow)已修好绘制尺寸 = **贴图原生 × SetDefaults scale**(与判定盒 w/h 解耦;src/entities/Arrow.ts 有 projSprite/projFrameImg/PROJ_ROT_RIGHT 旋转模式表先例,只读参考勿改该文件)。但敌方弹幕(bossAI_*.ts 家族、TownShot.ts、SquidCloud.ts 等)普遍是 `drawImage(img, -w/2, -h/2, this.w, this.h)` 式——把贴图归一到实体盒尺寸绘制。当贴图原生尺寸 ≠ 判定盒时视觉失真(小弹过大/大弹压扁),且未按 projframes 多帧行切片、未按 per-type 旋转模式(朝上 +π/2 vs 朝右 ToRotation,原版散在各 AI 的 rotation 赋值)。

# 实施要求
1. **全量盘点**:grep src/entities/*.ts 里 `drawImage(img, -this.w` 模式,列出每处实体→弹型→当前画法。
2. **逐款对账**:对每款弹查 SetDefaults(width/height/scale)+ Main.projFrames 帧数 + 贴图原生尺寸(terraria-assets/Images/Projectile_N.png,可用 node 读 PNG IHDR)+ 原版该弹 rotation 语义(源码 AI 段)。判定:当前画法是否失真(=原生尺寸×scale 与 w×h 差异显著,阈值>2×或纵横比翻转)。
3. **修复失真款**:改用"原生×scale+帧切片+正确旋转模式"。修复面**限 src/entities/bossAI_*.ts、TownShot.ts、SquidCloud.ts 及其它敌弹文件**——**禁碰 Arrow.ts / MissileProj.ts / WeaponProj.ts / Enemy.ts / Dart.ts / Game.ts / worldgen**(在途代理热区)。可从 Arrow.ts **import** 复用 projSprite/projFrameImg/projFrameCount/PROJ_ROT_RIGHT(已导出)。
4. 多帧行未动画化的款(恒帧 0)按 age/5 帧推进近似(Arrow 同款),备案注释。
5. 新增/修正测试:每修复款至少 1 断言(绘制尺寸/旋转模式用纯函数或离屏采样);vitest 定点绿。

# 纪律
- Edit 前重读;你改的文件 tsc 零错误(存量错误属其他会话);不跑全量测试。
- 诊断脚本(贴图尺寸盘点等)经 node tools/run-diag.mjs scripts/_xxx.mjs。

16	const spriteCache = new Map<number, HTMLImageElement>();
17	export function projSprite(projId: number): HTMLImageElement | null {
18	  let img = spriteCache.get(projId);

20	  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;

30	export function prefetchProjectiles(ids: Iterable<number>): void {

46	}

48	/** 单帧裁切缓存（id+帧号 → canvas），多帧行按帧高切片 */
49	const frameCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
50	export function projFrameImg(projId: number, frameIdx: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
51	  const img = projSprite(projId);
52	  if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return null;
53	  const frames = projFrameCount(projId);
54	  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(frames - 1, frameIdx));
55	  const fh = img.naturalHeight / frames;
56	  if (!Number.isFinite(fh) || fh < 1) return null;
57	  const key = `${projId}|${idx}`;
58	  let c = frameCache.get(key);
59	  if (c) return c;
60	  c = document.createElement('canvas');
61	  c.width = img.naturalWidth;
62	  c.height = Math.round(fh);
63	  const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
64	  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
65	  cx.drawImage(img, 0, Math.round(idx * fh), img.naturalWidth, Math.round(fh), 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
66	  if (frameCache.size > 2048) frameCache.clear();
67	  frameCache.set(key, c);
68	  return c;
69	}
70	
71	export interface ArrowOpts {
72	  /** 重力/tick（aiStyle1/2 = 0.3；直飞魔法弹传 0）。默认 0.3 */
73	  grav?: number;
74	  /** 原版 timeLeft（Projectile.cs:554 默认 1200） */
75	  life?: number;
76	  /** 穿透次数（原版 penetrate：手里剑 4、箭 1；-1 视作 1） */
77	  pierce?: number;
78	  /** 敌对弹（原版 Projectile.hostile，Damage_EVP :13708 门禁）：
79	   *  Boss/敌怪发射的弹传 true → 命中玩家结算伤害；玩家武器弹默认 false 不伤玩家。 */
80	  hostile?: boolean;
81	  /** aiStyle 14 弹跳弹（希腊火/装饰球等月事件弹幕，Projectile.cs 碰撞反弹
82	   *  cs:18314-18327 档）：撞实心块法向反弹 ×0.5 衰减而非消亡。 */
83	  bounce?: boolean;
84	  /** aiStyle 14 荆棘球档（世纪之花 277，Projectile.cs:18306-18314）：
85	   *  vx 恒反 ×0.9；仅入撞 |vy|>3 才竖弹 ×0.9（地面滚动语义）。 */
86	  thornBounce?: boolean;
87	  /** 延迟重力（世纪之花种子 275/276，Projectile.cs:54318-54329）：飞行满
88	   *  gravDelay tick 后才开始下坠（重力 0.025，非 aiStyle1 默认 0.3）。 */
89	  gravDelay?: number;
90	  /** 专家追踪（275/276/277 共用模式，Projectile.cs:54330-54345/:23307-23316）：
91	   *  每 tick v=(v*(weight-1)+dirToPlayer*speed)/weight，速度 <floor 归一到 floor
92	   *  （277 用 cap：>cap 归一到 cap）。spawn 侧仅在专家模式注入。 */
93	  homing?: { speed: number; weight: number; floor?: number; cap?: number };
94	  /** 原版 Projectile.extraUpdates（Projectile.cs:15331-15336 numUpdates 循环）：
95	   *  每逻辑帧把整段 AI/位移/碰撞/命中多跑 N 次——弹速视觉上 ×(N+1)，timeLeft
96	   *  同步按子步消耗（:15861 在循环内）。83 眼激光 SetDefaults=2（:1369）。 */
97	  extraUpdates?: number;
98	  /** X 轴空气阻力/tick（aiStyle 2 投掷族默认档 ×0.97，Projectile.cs:21969） */
99	  drag?: number;
100	  /** 终端下落速度（框架默认 16；aiStyle 2 投掷档 32，Projectile.cs:21973-21977） */
101	  maxFall?: number;
102	  /** 翻滚旋转（aiStyle 2 刀族：重力期内 rotation += (|vx|+|vy|)*0.03*dir，
103	   *  Projectile.cs:21508；前 gravDelay tick 保持 atan2 姿态 :21971-21972） */
104	  tumble?: boolean;
105	  /** 平飞期姿态锁定（48/54/93/520/599 前 20t atan2 姿态） */
106	  tumblePoseLock?: boolean;
107	  /** 泰拉刃光束 985（aiStyle 191，Player.cs:48316 出生注入）：
108	   *  ai[0]=朝向±1 / ai[1]=18（寿命=ai1+25=43t）/ ai[2]=物品 scale。
109	   *  淡入 ai1×0.5=9t、末 12t 淡出；34t 后 damage=0（纯视觉尾段）；减速 >8 档
110	   *  仅初速 >8 时激活（正牌出生速=瞄准向×5 恒不触发——973 甩剑才用） */
111	  terra?: { ai0: number; ai1: number; ai2: number };
112	  /** 星怒剑 503（aiStyle 5 :22139-22157）：targetY=目标线（鼠标 Y 与玩家
113	   *  cy−200 取小）；线上方穿墙/alpha 渐显钳 150，线下开始撞块 */
114	  star?: { targetY: number };
115	  /** 食人鱼 190（aiStyle 39，1156 食人鱼枪，GAP G3 行为层）：非空 = 走
116	   *  piranhaStep 独占状态机（直飞咬敌 → 咬住周期撕咬 → 松手返回回收），
117	   *  通用 subStep 的重力/撞块消亡/穿透递减语义不适用 */
118	  piranha?: PiranhaCtl;
119	}

121	/** 食人鱼控制面（Game 注入，同 PrismProj channelCb/aimCb 模式） */
122	export interface PiranhaCtl {
123	  /** Player.channel 电平（按住=持续咬；松手 → ai[0]=1 返回 + ai[1]=−1 禁再咬，
124	   *  Projectile.cs:26093-26096）。附带 heldItem/死亡门（同 FlailProj 回调先例） */
125	  channel: () => boolean;
126	  /** 当前瞄准点（世界坐标）——回收补弹的出生方向（ItemCheck_Shoot num4/num5） */
127	  aim: () => { x: number; y: number };
128	}

131	 *  penetrate −1 / ranged / timeLeft 走通用默认 3600 :526 /
132	 *  usesLocalNPCImmunity + localNPCHitCooldown = 14 :2520-2521） */
133	export const PIRANHA_PROJ = 190;
134	export const PIRANHA_LIFE = 3600;

154	 *  NUM_FIELDS 提取，249 款非 0；83 眼激光=2 等原先手工条目同源于 SetDefaults） */

156	/** 旋转模式（scripts/_projrot-audit.mjs 对 AI_001 type 链逐分支提取 + 非 aiStyle1
157	 *  特例）：默认 'up' = 贴图朝上（AI_001 尾部默认 atan2+π/2，:54877——箭/子弹）；
158	 *  下表 = 贴图【朝右】的弹型（rotation=atan2(vy,vx)，向左运动时按原版
159	 *  spriteDirection 水平镜像，食人鱼 AI 即 :26122-26140 模式）：
160	 *  16 魔法飞弹头（113 Magic Missile，AI_009 :54039 ToRotation；54×54 方图头朝右）、
161	 *  34 Flamelash 火鞭（218，AI_020 族 ToRotation；48×384 八帧行按帧行切片）、
162	 *  190 食人鱼（aiStyle 39，1156 食人鱼枪；曾恒 +π/2 → 鱼 90° 侧翻）、
163	 *  837（AI_001 显式 MIRROR 分支 :54715，1313 骷髅头法书 shoot）、
164	 *  1023（AI_001 仅 wiggle :54743，基姿态 0 朝右，5460 发射器） */
165	const PROJ_ROT_RIGHT = new Set([16, 34, 190, 837, 1023]);
166	export { PROJ_ROT_RIGHT };

169	  w = 10; h = 10; // 原版 SetDefaults type 1：width/height = 10；构造器按弹型覆写

171	  vy: number;
172	  damage: number;
173	  knockback: number;

176	  /** 绘制 scale（SetDefaults scale 字段；绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生 × scale，
177	   *  与判定盒 w/h 无关——子弹 14 是 2×20 曳光条 × 1.2，曾误画成 10×100） */
178	  drawScale = 1;
179	  /** 回收掉落的 item key（null = 不回收，如燃烧箭） */

182	  life: number;
183	  pierce: number;
184	  /** 发射时 maxPenetrate（穿透判定用——剩 1 的穿透弹仍是穿透语义,Projectile.cs:11904） */
185	  pierceInit: number;

187	  hostile: boolean;
188	  /** 随从/哨兵射出的弹（ProjectileID.Sets.MinionShot/SentryShot 语义：吃鞭 tag） */
189	  whipTagShot = false;
190	  /** 命中施加 OnFire 300t（1106 火舌 :11002-11004） */
191	  ignite = false;
192	  /** 暴击加成（百分点，spawn 侧注入：player.critChance(kind)+item.crit；基 4% 另计。
193	   *  审计 §6：此前硬编码 4% 导致远程/魔法/投掷吃不到装备/套装/词缀/item.crit */
194	  critBonus = 0;
195	  /** 暴击总概率阈值（0-1，spawn 侧一次性算好；未设=按 critBonus+4%） */
196	  critChance = 0;
197	  /** 护甲穿透（N:81913 减防语义——spawn 注入，命中时传 hurt 第 5 参） */
198	  armorPen = 0;
199	  /** 星云套 booster 回调（StrikeNPC :12892-12905：魔法弹命中 → Game 判定掉落） */
200	  nebulaCb: (() => void) | null = null;
201	  /** aiStyle 14 弹跳：撞块反弹不消亡 */
202	  bounce: boolean;
203	  /** 冰霜盔甲引擎资格（ranged 弹 true;魔法/召唤弹 false——原版 melee||ranged 门） */
204	  frostEligible = false;
205	  /** aiStyle 14 荆棘球档（277）：撞块按 Projectile.cs:18306-18314 反弹 */
206	  thornBounce: boolean;
207	  /** 延迟重力（275/276）：age 超过此值才施加 grav；-1=不延迟 */
208	  gravDelay: number;
209	  /** 专家追踪参数（null=无） */

211	  /** 原版 Projectile.extraUpdates：每帧额外子步数（0=普通 1 步） */
212	  extraUpdates: number;
213	  /** X 轴空气阻力/tick（1=无；投掷族 0.97） */
214	  drag: number;
215	  /** 终端下落速度（缺省 16） */
216	  maxFall: number;
217	  /** 翻滚旋转（aiStyle 2 刀族重力期） */

225	  // ---- 泰拉刃光束 985（aiStyle 191）专属态 ----
226	  terra = false;
227	  // ---- 星怒剑 503 专属态 ----
228	  /** 目标线 Y（null=非星怒弹）；cy>targetY 后 tileCollide 生效（:22139-22143） */
229	  starY: number | null = null;
230	  /** alpha（255 起 −15/t；线上钳 150 / 线下钳 0——:22197-22206） */
231	  starAlpha = 255;
232	  /** 周期音（Item9 :22177-22180，20-60t 掷一次） */
233	  private starSndT = 20;
234	  /** tileCollide 已生效（越目标线后） */
235	  private starCollide = false;
236	  /** localAI[0] 计时 */
237	  terraT = 0;
238	  /** ai[0] 朝向（±1）/ ai[1] 生命基准 18 / ai[2] 物品 scale（出生注入） */
239	  terraAi0 = 1;
240	  terraAi1 = 18;
241	  terraAi2 = 1;
242	  /** 尾段清伤（t ≥ Lerp(ai1, ai1+25, 0.65) → damage=0，:39337-39340） */
243	  terraNoHit = false;
244	  /** localAI[1] 近墙减速标记 */
245	  terraWall = false;
246	  /** 已反射（原版 Projectile.reflected：反射源命中后置位，防重复反射；
247	   *  反射后弹体转 hostile——不再入下方敌怪判定，改走 hitPlayer 伤玩家） */
248	  reflected = false;
249	  /** 穿透投射物的同敌免疫表（敌人 id 集合） */
250	  private hitSet = new Set<number>();
251	  // ---- 喵刀弹 502（Meowmere Cat）专属态 ----
252	  /** ai[0]：弹跳+命中共享计数（0..5，≥5 消亡）——弹跳 :18169 递增/命中 :16794
253	   *  递增同槽（先到 5 者杀弹，喵叫音高 style=5+ai0 随之爬升） */
254	  meowCount = 0;
255	  /** localNPCHitCooldown 同敌再命中冷却（SetDefaults :5466-5468 = 10t） */
256	  private meowHitCd = new Map<number, number>();
257	  /** oldPos 拖尾环（彩虹拖尾 Main.cs:32495-32513 沿 oldPos 逐段绘制，N=10） */
258	  private meowTrail: number[] = [];
259	  /** 迪斯科 RGB（DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB Main.cs:19441 六段 ±7/帧；初值 R=255） */
260	  private discoR = 255;
261	  private discoG = 0;
262	  private discoB = 0;
263	  private discoStyle = 0;
264	  /** 弹体点光（Game 实体光扫读 lightRGB——同 Enemy 约定） */
265	  lightRGB: [number, number, number] | null = null;
266	  /** 点光源像素覆盖（缺省=实体中心；985 泰拉刃 :39382 光心=弹心+朝向 85×scale） */
267	  lightRGBAt: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
268	  // ---- 食人鱼 190（aiStyle 39，Projectile.cs:26065-26315）专属态 ----
269	  /** 非空 = 食人鱼状态机激活（fixedUpdate 直入 piranhaStep） */
270	  piranha: PiranhaCtl | null = null;
271	  /** alpha（SetDefaults :2517 = 255；AI :26067 每 tick −50，6t 淡入归零） */
272	  pAlpha = 255;
273	  /** ai[0]：0=直飞（:26245 分支）/ 1=返回玩家（:26266 分支） */
274	  pAi0 = 0;
275	  /** ai[1]：0=未咬（命中即咬 :12447）/ >0=咬住目标 / −1=松手禁再咬（:26095） */
276	  pAi1 = 0;
277	  /** 咬住目标（vanilla 存 npc index+1，此处直接持引用 + 每 tick 校验存活） */
278	  pTarget: PiranhaTarget | null = null;
279	  /** localNPCImmunity 同敌冷却表（SetDefaults :2521 = 14t，:13157 命中写入） */
280	  private pHitCd = new Map<number, number>();
281	  /** 帧推进计数 / 当前帧（:26301-26310：4t/帧 4 帧循环——draw 侧消费） */
282	  pFrameCounter = 0;
283	  pFrame = 0;
284	  /** 姿态角（未镜像系 atan2(vy,vx)）与翻转位（spriteDirection<0）——咬住时
285	   *  velocity 已清零，atan2(0,0) 会把鱼掰回 0°，故由 AI 侧在清速前记录（:26119-26143） */
286	  pRot = 0;
287	  pFlip = false;
288	  /** 出生弹速（ItemCheck_Shoot num4/num5 模长 = item.shootSpeed 14）——补弹复用 */
289	  pShootSpeed = 0;
290	  dead = false;
291	
292	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number,
293	    knockback: number, projId = 1, dropKey: string | null = null, opts?: ArrowOpts) {
294	    super();
295	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
296	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
297	    this.damage = damage;
298	    this.knockback = knockback;
299	    this.projId = projId;
300	    this.dropKey = dropKey;
301	    // 判定盒/绘制 scale 按弹型取 SetDefaults（子弹 14 = 4×4 hitbox；箭 1 = 10×10）
302	    const pd0 = projectileData(projId);
303	    if (pd0?.width) this.w = pd0.width;
304	    if (pd0?.height) this.h = pd0.height;
305	    this.drawScale = pd0?.scale ?? 1;
306	    this.grav = opts?.grav ?? 0.3;
307	    this.life = opts?.life ?? 1200;
308	    this.pierce = opts?.pierce ?? 1;
309	    this.pierceInit = this.pierce;
310	    this.hostile = opts?.hostile ?? false;
311	    this.bounce = opts?.bounce ?? false;
312	    this.thornBounce = opts?.thornBounce ?? false;
313	    this.gravDelay = opts?.gravDelay ?? -1;
314	    this.homing = opts?.homing ?? null;
315	    this.extraUpdates = opts?.extraUpdates ?? pd0?.extraUpdates ?? 0;   // 子弹 14=1(2 步/tick)、高速弹 207=2
316	    this.drag = opts?.drag ?? 1;
317	    this.maxFall = opts?.maxFall ?? 16;
318	    this.tumble = opts?.tumble ?? false;
319	    this.tumblePoseLock = opts?.tumblePoseLock ?? false;
320	    this.terra = !!opts?.terra;
321	    if (opts?.terra) {
322	      this.terraAi0 = opts.terra.ai0;
323	      this.terraAi1 = opts.terra.ai1;
324	      this.terraAi2 = opts.terra.ai2;
325	    }
326	    if (opts?.star) this.starY = opts.star.targetY;
327	    if (opts?.piranha) {
328	      this.piranha = opts.piranha;
329	      this.pShootSpeed = Math.hypot(vx, vy);   // Game 侧可覆写为精确 shootSpeed
330	    }
331	  }
332	
333	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
334	    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标——
335	    // 曾双变换把箭甩出屏幕,表现为"箭隐形但能命中")
336	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
337	    if (!ctx) return;
338	    // 泰拉刃光束 985 独占（DrawProj_TerraBlade2Shot Main.cs:27670-27712）：
339	    // Frame(1,4) 四帧 170×170（帧 0 主体/帧 3 白芯）；多层镜像叠画：
340	    // 蓝(45,124,205)×2 对称 ±π/8 张开 + 绿白内芯 ×2 + 绿(34,177,76) ×2
341	    // ±0.25 收拢 + 黄绿(181,230,29) + 帧 3 白芯三层递缩——ai[0]=0（未传参）
342	    // 时各 ±ai0 项归零对称；旋转角=velocity 角（无 +π/2，贴图正交）。
343	    // 淡出 = Remap(localAI[0],0,25,1,0)
344	    if (this.terra) {
345	      const img = projSprite(985);
346	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
347	      const fw = img.naturalWidth, fh = img.naturalHeight / 4;
348	      // 淡入淡出（:39344 985 专属）：Remap(t,0,ai1/2,0,1)×Remap(t,ai1+13,ai1+25,1,0)
349	      const aIn = Math.min(1, this.terraT / (this.terraAi1 * 0.5));
350	      const aOut = this.terraT <= this.terraAi1 + 13 ? 1
351	        : Math.max(0, (this.terraAi1 + 25 - this.terraT) / 12);
352	      const fade = aIn * aOut;
353	      // num6 双段（:39273 主段 Remap(t,ai1*0.4,ai1+25,0,1)——绘制张合的推进分母）
354	      const num = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (this.terraT - this.terraAi1 * 0.4) / (this.terraAi1 + 25 - this.terraAi1 * 0.4)));
355	      const spread = 1 - num;
356	      // 绘制 scale（:39341）：Remap(1−(1−num6)²,0,1,1.5,1)×ai2
357	      const fv = 1 - (1 - num) * (1 - num);
358	      const terraScale = (1.5 - 0.5 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, fv))) * this.terraAi2;
359	      const rot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
360	      const layer = (sy: number, a: number, angOff: number, frame: number, flipV: boolean) => {
361	        ctx.save();
362	        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, a * fade));
363	        ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
364	        ctx.rotate(rot + angOff * this.terraAi0);   // ±ai[0] 方向项（:27694 ai0=±1）
365	        if (flipV) ctx.scale(1, -1);
366	        const dw = fw * sy * terraScale;
367	        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, frame * fh, fw, fh, -dw / 2, -fh * sy * terraScale / 2, dw, fh * sy * terraScale);
368	        ctx.restore();
369	        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
370	      };
371	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
372	      layer(0.95, 0.5, Math.PI / 8 * spread, 0, false);            // 蓝镜像对（±π/8×spread）
373	      layer(0.95, 0.5, -Math.PI / 8 * spread, 0, true);
374	      layer(1, 0.12, 0, 0, false);                                 // 绿白内芯（color5 近似）
375	      layer(1, 0.3, Math.PI / 4 * 0.25 * spread, 0, false);        // 绿 ±0.25 收拢
376	      layer(1, 0.3, -Math.PI / 4 * 0.25 * spread, 0, true);
377	      layer(0.975, 0.5, Math.PI / 4 * 0.15 * spread, 0, false);    // 黄绿主芯
378	      layer(1, 0.6, Math.PI / 4 * 0.05 * spread, 3, false);        // 帧 3 白芯三层
379	      layer(0.8, 0.5, -0.05, 3, false);
380	      layer(0.6, 0.4, -0.1, 3, false);
381	      return;
382	    }
383	    // 星怒剑 503 独占：rotation=vel−π/2（:22236-22239），origin (w/2, 70)
384	    // （Main.cs:29898-29901——34×90 贴图锚点近底部，剑尖前伸 70px）；
385	    // Opacity = 1−alpha/255 渐显
386	    if (this.starY !== null) {
387	      const img = projSprite(503);
388	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
389	      ctx.save();
390	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - this.starAlpha / 255));
391	      ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
392	      ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) - Math.PI / 2);
393	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
394	      ctx.drawImage(img, -img.naturalWidth / 2, -70, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight);
395	      ctx.restore();
396	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
397	      return;
398	    }
399	    // 翻滚态用累积角（aiStyle 2）；48/54/93/520/599 平飞期姿态锁定 atan2
400	    const tumbling = this.tumble && !(this.tumblePoseLock && this.age <= this.gravDelay);
401	    // 食人鱼姿态由 AI 侧维护（pRot/pFlip）：咬住时 velocity 清零（:26126-26129），
402	    // 现算 atan2(0,0)=0 会把鱼掰回正右——原版 rotation 在清速前取目标向（:26119-26143）
403	    const ang = this.piranha ? this.pRot
404	      : (tumbling ? this.tumbleRot : Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx));
405	    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
406	    // 喵刀弹 502 彩虹拖尾（Main.cs:32495-32513）：Projectile_250.png（14×32）沿
407	    // oldPos 逐段——旋转=段向-π/2、纵向拉伸=段长/32、alpha=127/255×(1-i/N)，
408	    // 画在【本体之前】（原版在本体绘制后叠画，此处先画拖尾再本体等效）
409	    if (this.projId === 502 && this.meowTrail.length >= 4) {
410	      const streak = projSprite(250);
411	      if (streak && streak.complete && streak.naturalWidth > 0) {
412	        const N = this.meowTrail.length / 2;
413	        for (let i = N - 1; i > 0; i--) {
414	          const x1 = this.meowTrail[i * 2], y1 = this.meowTrail[i * 2 + 1];
415	          const x2 = this.meowTrail[(i - 1) * 2], y2 = this.meowTrail[(i - 1) * 2 + 1];
416	          const segLen = Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
417	          if (segLen < 0.01) continue;
418	          const segAng = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1) - Math.PI / 2;
419	          ctx.save();
420	          ctx.translate(x1 + this.w / 2, y1 + this.h / 2);
421	          ctx.rotate(segAng);
422	          ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
423	          ctx.globalAlpha = (127 / 255) * (1 - i / N);
424	          // origin (w/2, 0)：段起点为锚、沿段向拉伸到段长（scaleY=段长/贴图高）
425	          ctx.drawImage(streak, -streak.naturalWidth / 2, 0,
426	            streak.naturalWidth, Math.max(1, segLen));
427	          ctx.restore();
428	          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
429	        }
430	      }
431	    }
432	    ctx.save();
433	    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
434	    // 朝右贴图族（PROJ_ROT_RIGHT）：rotation=atan2(vy,vx)（vanilla :26122-26140 模式），
435	    // 向左运动水平镜像（spriteDirection 语义）；其余默认朝上 atan2+π/2（AI_001 L54877）
436	    const rightArt = PROJ_ROT_RIGHT.has(this.projId);
437	    const flipLeft = this.piranha ? this.pFlip : this.vx < 0;
438	    if (rightArt && flipLeft) {
439	      ctx.scale(-1, 1);              // 先镜像再旋转（R(π−ang)∘M ≡ 原版 flip+atan2(−vy,−vx)）
440	      ctx.rotate(Math.PI - ang);
441	    } else {
442	      ctx.rotate(rightArt ? ang : ang + Math.PI / 2);
443	    }
444	    // 502 朝向（:22659-22665）：spriteDirection=sign(vx)——贴图在旋转坐标系内水平镜像
445	    if (this.projId === 502 && this.vx < 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
446	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
447	    // 食人鱼淡入（aiStyle 39 :26067-26071：alpha 255 起 −50/t，6t 归零）
448	    if (this.piranha) ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - this.pAlpha / 255));
449	    // 多帧行（190 食人鱼 4 帧/837 3 帧）按帧行切片防胶片压扁；单帧走整图。
450	    // 食人鱼帧号由 AI 推进（:26301-26310 frameCounter≥4 推帧 4 帧循环），
451	    // 其余型号沿用 age/5 档
452	    const frames = projFrameCount(this.projId);
453	    const fIdx = this.piranha ? this.pFrame : Math.floor(this.age / 5) % frames;
454	    const frameImg = frames > 1 ? projFrameImg(this.projId, fIdx) : null;
455	    const drawImg = frameImg ?? img;
456	    if (drawImg && (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).complete !== false
457	      && (drawImg.width > 0 || (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth > 0)) {
458	      // 原生尺寸 × SetDefaults scale（与判定盒 w/h 解耦）：子弹 14 = 2×20×1.2
459	      // 曳光条；箭 1 = 14×32 贴图 × 1。曾统一拉成 w×w 宽 → 100px 巨型子弹/箭偏小
460	      const s = this.drawScale;
461	      const iw = drawImg.width || (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth;
462	      const ih = drawImg.height || (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalHeight;
463	      ctx.drawImage(drawImg as CanvasImageSource, -iw * s / 2, -ih * s / 2, iw * s, ih * s);
464	    } else {
465	      // 贴图未就绪：短线兜底
466	      ctx.strokeStyle = this.projId === 2 ? '#FFB060' : '#D8C8A0';
467	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
468	      ctx.beginPath();
469	      ctx.moveTo(0, 0);
470	      ctx.lineTo(0, -Math.min(14, Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) * 1.4));
471	      ctx.stroke();
472	    }
473	    ctx.restore();
474	  }
475	
476	  /** 统一消亡出口（Projectile.Kill 语义钩子）：碎镜 1085 近身 150px 坏运等
477	   *  按弹型分发由 Game 侧 onProjectileDeath 消费 */
478	  private killNow(game: GameHooks): void {
479	    this.dead = true;
480	    game.onProjectileDeath?.(this.projId, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
481	  }
482	
483	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
484	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：对敌判定归拥有者客户端；对玩家（hostile）由访客本地结算 Damage_EVP（netPuppetProjContact）
485	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
486	    // 食人鱼 190 独占状态机（aiStyle 39）——重力/穿透递减/撞块消亡等通用语义不适用
487	    if (this.piranha) { this.piranhaStep(game); return; }
488	    // extraUpdates 子步循环（Projectile.cs:15331-15336）：numUpdates = extraUpdates，
489	    // while(numUpdates>=0) 跑完整 AI/位移/命中体——timeLeft 也在循环内逐子步消耗
490	    //（:15861），故弹体寿命同步缩短为 1/(N+1)。
491	    for (let u = 0; u <= this.extraUpdates; u++) {
492	      this.subStep(game);
493	      if (this.dead) return;
494	    }
495	  }
496	
497	  /** 单次子步（无 extraUpdates 时即整帧本体） */
498	  private subStep(game: GameHooks): void {
499	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
500	    const world = game.world;
501	    this.age++;
502	    // aiStyle 1/2 通用重力（原版箭 0.3/tick 抛物线；直飞弹 grav=0）；
503	    // 275/276 延迟重力（Projectile.cs:54318-54329：35t 后 g=0.025）；
504	    // aiStyle 2 投掷族（Projectile.cs:21955-21977 默认档）：20t 平飞后
505	    // vy+=0.4、vx×=0.97，终端 32
506	    if (this.grav !== 0 && (this.gravDelay < 0 || this.age > this.gravDelay)) {
507	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + this.grav, this.maxFall);
508	      if (this.drag !== 1) this.vx *= this.drag;
509	    }
510	    // 翻滚（aiStyle 2 刀族 :21508，自出生每 tick 累加）；48/54/93/520/599 在
511	    // 平飞期由 draw 侧姿态锁定覆盖（:21971-21972）
512	    if (this.tumble) {
513	      this.tumbleRot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
514	    }
515	    // 泰拉刃光束 985（AI_191 :39248-39266 + 985 专属尾段 :39333-39377）：
516	    // 寿命 = ai[1]+25（出生 18 → 43t）；淡入 ai[1]/2=9t、末 12t 淡出（:39344）；
517	    // t≥Lerp(ai1,ai1+25,0.65)≈34 清伤（纯视觉尾段）；>8 速才减速/探墙（正牌
518	    // 出生速=瞄准向×5 恒不触发，973 甩剑共用段）
519	    if (this.terra) {
520	      this.terraT++;
521	      const terraLife = this.terraAi1 + 25;
522	      if (this.terraT >= terraLife) { this.killNow(game); return; }
523	      if (!this.terraNoHit && this.terraT >= Math.round(this.terraAi1 + 25 * 0.65)) {
524	        this.terraNoHit = true;
525	        this.damage = 0;                                  // :39337-39340 damage=0
526	      }
527	      const stT = world.store;
528	      this.lightRGB = [0.25, 0.86, 0.38];    // Color(64,220,96)（:39382）
529	      // 光心 = 弹心 + 朝向 ×85×scale（:39382 Center+rotation.ToRotationVector2()*85*scale）
530	      {
531	        const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) || 1;
532	        this.lightRGBAt = { x: this.cx + (this.vx / sp) * 85, y: this.cy + (this.vy / sp) * 85 };
533	      }
534	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 0.5 && Math.random() < 0.6) {
535	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#7CE81E', 1, 1.2, { life: 18 });
536	      }
537	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 8) {
538	        this.vx *= 0.94; this.vy *= 0.94;
539	        if (!this.terraWall) {
540	          const rot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
541	          let clear = false;
542	          for (let k = -1; k <= 1 && !clear; k += 0.5) {
543	            const a = rot + k * (Math.PI / 4) * 0.25;
544	            clear = canHit(stT, this.cx, this.cy, 0, 0,
545	              this.cx + Math.cos(a) * 110, this.cy + Math.sin(a) * 110, 0, 0);
546	          }
547	          if (!clear) this.terraWall = true;
548	        }
549	        if (this.terraWall && Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 8) { this.vx *= 0.8; this.vy *= 0.8; }
550	        if (this.terraWall) { this.vx *= 0.88; this.vy *= 0.88; }
551	      }
552	    }
553	    // 星怒剑 503（aiStyle 5 :22139-22157 + 503 段 :22197-22266）：无重力恒速；
554	    // 线上方穿墙（tileCollide=false）→ 越线后撞块；alpha 255−15/t 渐显（线上
555	    // 钳 150 / 线下钳 0）；周期 Item9 星啸；尘 58 星尘尾；rotation=vel−π/2（draw 侧）
556	    if (this.starY !== null) {
557	      // :22139-22143 tileCollide=true 仅当 Center.Y > ai[1]（严格大于——边界对齐）
558	      this.starCollide = this.cy > this.starY;
559	      const above = this.cy < this.starY;
560	      const floorA = above ? 150 : 0;
561	      this.starAlpha = Math.max(floorA, this.starAlpha - 15);
562	      if (--this.starSndT <= 0) {
563	        this.starSndT = 20 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40);
564	        game.playSfxFiles(['Item_9'], 0.5, this.cx, this.cy);
565	      }
566	      if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
567	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FFF8C8', 1, 0.9, { life: 20 });
568	      }
569	    }
570	    // 专家追踪（Projectile.cs:54330-54345 275/276 / :23307-23316 277：
571	    // v=(v*(weight-1)+dirToPlayer*speed)/weight；<floor 或 >cap 归一）
572	    if (this.homing) {
573	      const p = game.player;
574	      if (p && !p.dead) {
575	        let dx = p.cx - (this.x + this.w / 2), dy = p.cy - (this.y + this.h / 2);
576	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
577	        dx = dx / d * this.homing.speed;
578	        dy = dy / d * this.homing.speed;
579	        this.vx = (this.vx * (this.homing.weight - 1) + dx) / this.homing.weight;
580	        this.vy = (this.vy * (this.homing.weight - 1) + dy) / this.homing.weight;
581	        const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
582	        if (this.homing.floor !== undefined && sp < this.homing.floor && sp > 0) {
583	          this.vx = this.vx / sp * this.homing.floor;
584	          this.vy = this.vy / sp * this.homing.floor;
585	        }
586	        if (this.homing.cap !== undefined && sp > this.homing.cap) {
587	          this.vx = this.vx / sp * this.homing.cap;
588	          this.vy = this.vy / sp * this.homing.cap;
589	        }
590	      }
591	    }
592	    this.x += this.vx;
593	    this.y += this.vy;
594	    // 喵刀弹 502：迪斯科点光（Projectile.cs:22613-22621 (Disco+0.5)/2）+ 拖尾采样
595	    if (this.projId === 502) {
596	      const n = 7;   // DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB（Main.cs:19441-19496 六段 ±7/帧）
597	      switch (this.discoStyle) {
598	        case 0: this.discoG = Math.min(255, this.discoG + n); if (this.discoG >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
599	        case 1: this.discoR = Math.max(0, this.discoR - n); if (this.discoR <= 0) this.discoStyle++; break;
600	        case 2: this.discoB = Math.min(255, this.discoB + n); if (this.discoB >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
601	        case 3: this.discoG = Math.max(0, this.discoG - n); if (this.discoG <= 0) this.discoStyle++; break;
602	        case 4: this.discoR = Math.min(255, this.discoR + n); if (this.discoR >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
603	        default: this.discoB = Math.max(0, this.discoB - n); if (this.discoB <= 0) this.discoStyle = 0; break;
604	      }

606	      this.meowTrail.push(this.x, this.y);
607	      if (this.meowTrail.length > 20) this.meowTrail.splice(0, this.meowTrail.length - 20);   // oldPos 10 点
608	      for (const [k, v] of this.meowHitCd) {
609	        if (v <= 1) this.meowHitCd.delete(k); else this.meowHitCd.set(k, v - 1);
610	      }
611	    }
612	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
613	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
614	    if (!world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.killNow(game); return; }
615	    const tileType = world.store.get(tx, ty);
616	    // 星怒剑线上方穿墙（:22139-22143 越线前 tileCollide=false）——整段跳过
617	    if (tileType !== 0 && !(this.starY !== null && !this.starCollide)) {
618	      // 可砍物（杂草/瓦罐）：Projectile.CutTiles 语义——弹幕扫过即砍
619	      game.cutTile(tx, ty);
620	      // 阻挡判定只看【实心】(Main.tileSolid 语义):树干/火把/平台等非实心格
621	      // 箭直接穿过(此前 tileType!==0 一刀切,箭会被树挡住——树 solid:false)
622	      if (!world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) return;

624	      if (this.bounce && this.bounceOff(game)) return;
625	      // 喵刀弹 502（AI_008 弹跳档 :18165-18206）：tink 音 + ai[0] 计数 ≥5 消亡；

632	      if (this.dropKey && Math.random() < 1 / 3) game.spawnDrop(this.x, this.y, this.dropKey, 1);

635	    }
636	    // 敌对弹命中玩家（原版 Projectile.Damage_EVP :13706-13830）：不消耗弹体，
637	    // Player.damage 自带 iframes 去重（= 原版 player.immune 门禁）；
638	    // 实际造成伤害才走 StatusPlayer 的 debuff 授予（:13798-13800；
639	    // 276 毒种子的中毒授予在 statusPlayer case 276）
640	    if (this.hostile && hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage)) statusPlayer(game, this.projId);

642	    // 不消耗弹体(原版该分支不动 keepIterating)
643	    if (this.hostile) hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, 0, 'hostile');

645	    // ——不再伤小动物/敌怪，只走上方 hitPlayer 回打发射者

647	    // 小动物:一击致死并消耗弹体(原版小动物是 NPC;近战挥砍同语义)

649	    // 命中敌人（原版 penetrate：箭 1 射中即停；手里剑 4 穿 4 敌，同敌免疫防连击）
650	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
651	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; id: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks, pen?: number, crit?: boolean, pierce?: number, fromPlayer?: boolean, penPercent?: number) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] }; reflectsProjectiles?: boolean };

654	        // 反射源（419 旋刃冲刺等 reflectsProjectiles 置标）：命中前先走反射
655	        // （原版 Projectile.cs:12050-12060，反射即终止本帧命中迭代 keepIterating=false）
656	        if (tryReflectProjectile(this, this.projId, e, game)) {
657	          this.pierce = 1;            // 原版 penetrate=1（NPC.cs:67058）
658	          this.pierceInit = 1;        // 反射后按单发弹语义（命中不设敌方帧）
659	          this.hitSet.clear();        // 转敌对后命中表作废
660	          return;
661	        }
662	        // 502 喵刀弹：localNPCHitCooldown=10t 同敌再命中（穿透弹 hitSet 是永久免疫，
663	        // 502 是"同一敌 10t 冷却后可再咬"——SetDefaults :5466-5468）
664	        if (this.projId === 502) {
665	          const cd = this.meowHitCd.get(e.id) ?? 0;
666	          if (cd > 0) continue;
667	          this.meowHitCd.set(e.id, 10);
668	        } else if (this.hitSet.has(e.id)) continue; // 穿透弹已命中过此敌
669	        // ★鞭 tag（WhipTagEffect.CanRunHitEffects :73-84：MinionShot/SentryShot 弹幕吃
670	        //   tag——随从/哨兵射出的箭 whipTagShot 置标时走 resolveWhipTagHit 统一结算：
671	        //   ModifyTaggedHit tag 加伤+TagCrit 暴击（WhipTagEffect.cs:58-70）、OnTaggedHit
672	        //   （黑收成 916）、proc 窗口首跳（TagEffectState.cs:216-243）。随从弹无基础
673	        //   暴击（原版 summon 系 crit 仅来自 TagCrit）——4% 基础暴击只留给玩家远程弹）
674	        let dmg = this.damage;
675	        let crit: boolean;
676	        if (this.whipTagShot) {
677	          const en2 = e as unknown as import('./WhipTag').WhipTagged;
678	          // 黑收成 OnTaggedHit 直伤通道（916 等价：额外一跳落在被命中敌上）
679	          en2.hurtFx = (d: number) => { e.hurt(d, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, -2, game); };
680	          const res = resolveWhipTagHit(en2, dmg, game,
681	            (el) => (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(el, 'projectiles'),
682	            SUMMON_TAG_MUL[this.projId] ?? 1, e.x + e.w / 2, e.y + e.h / 2);
683	          dmg = res.dmg;
684	          crit = res.crit;
685	        } else {
686	          // 暴击：基 4% + spawn 注入的系暴击/item.crit（P:2300-2304/P:25230-25232）
687	          crit = Math.random() < (this.critChance || (4 + this.critBonus) / 100);
688	          if (crit) dmg *= 2;
689	        }
690	        if (this.ignite) {
691	          const en3 = e as unknown as { onFireT?: number };
692	          en3.onFireT = Math.max(en3.onFireT ?? 0, 5); // AddBuff(24,300)=5s
693	        }
694	        // 命中 debuff（StatusNPC:10555 表驱动——火枪弹 2 33% OnFire 180t 等,按型号掷骰）
695	        applyProjStatus(this.projId, e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
696	        // 冰霜盔甲引擎(ranged 门,:93712;魔法/召唤弹 frostEligible=false)
697	        if (this.frostEligible) {
698	          applyFrostBurn((game.player as unknown as { equipStats: { frostBurn: boolean } }).equipStats.frostBurn,
699	            e as unknown as Record<string, number>);
700	        }
701	        if (this.nebulaCb) this.nebulaCb(); // 星云 booster（:12892-12905，伤害>0 门已过）
702	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
703	        e.hurt(dmg, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, this.armorPen, crit, this.pierceInit, true);
704	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, Math.round(dmg), crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD060');
705	        this.hitSet.add(e.id);
706	        // 502 喵刀弹命中：无特效音/无计数消亡（全库唯一 PlaySound(37) 在撞块
707	        // HandleMovement :16797——2026-08-13 实测复核修正：曾误接在命中链）。
708	        // 命中只消费 penetrate（5 NPC 上限）+ localNPCHitCooldown 同敌冷却
709	        // ---- 吸血链（Projectile.cs:12877-12892 on-hit 段）----
710	        // 吸血鬼飞刀 304 → vampireHeal（:12879-12882，!moonLeech 门）；
711	        // 魔法弹 + 幽灵套 ghostHeal → ghostHeal（:12883-12888，canGhostHeal 全 true
712	        // 近似——雕像产怪 flag 未实装）。ghostHurt（幽灵伤害头 156）未接，登记。
713	        // numHits = 含本次的命中数（原版 StrikeNPC 先自增，hitSet.size 此时已含本敌）
714	        if (this.projId === 304) {
715	          game.applyLifeSteal?.('vampire', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y);
716	        } else if (projectileData(this.projId)?.magic) {
717	          game.applyLifeSteal?.('ghost', Math.round(dmg), this.hitSet.size, this.x, this.y, true);
718	        }
719	        if (--this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
720	      }
721	    }
722	    // 城镇 NPC 受击(原版 Projectile.Damage 对 friendly NPC 同样生效——杀裁缝师
723	    // 召骷髅王即此链);穿透/销毁语义与敌怪一致
724	    if (hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2)
725	      && --this.pierce <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
726	  }
727	
728	  /** 荆棘球 277 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18306-18314）：vx 恒反 ×0.9；
729	   *  竖向仅入撞 |vy|>3 才 ×-0.9（否则贴地滚动）。逐轴回退探测，脱困失败 false。 */
730	  private thornBounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
731	    const st = game.world.store;
732	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
733	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
734	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
735	    };
736	    let bounced = false;
737	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
738	      this.x -= this.vx;
739	      this.vx *= -0.9;
740	      bounced = true;
741	    }
742	    if (this.vy > 3 && !solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
743	      this.y -= this.vy;
744	      this.vy *= -0.9;
745	      bounced = true;
746	    }
747	    return bounced;
748	  }
749	
750	  /** aiStyle 14 撞块反弹（Projectile.cs:18314-18327 默认档：法向 ×-0.5，低速归零）。
751	   *  逐轴回退探测穿透轴并反弹；两轴均无法脱困（嵌入过深）则返回 false 交还消亡。 */
752	  private bounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
753	    const st = game.world.store;
754	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
755	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
756	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
757	    };
758	    let bounced = false;
759	    // X 轴：回退一步脱离实心 → 穿透轴是 X，反弹并退回
760	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
761	      this.x -= this.vx;
762	      this.vx *= -0.5;
763	      bounced = true;
764	    }
765	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
766	      this.y -= this.vy;
767	      this.vy = this.vy > 1 ? -this.vy * 0.5 : 0;
768	      bounced = true;
769	    }
770	    return bounced;
771	  }
772	
773	  /** 喵刀弹 502 撞块档（HandleMovement :16794-16812——type 502 专支，先于
774	   *  通用 aiStyle-8 tink 链（:18165）命中，故弹跳只喵叫不 tink）：
775	   *  ai[0]=Clamp(+1,1,5) + PlaySound(37=Meowmere, style 5+ai0)（Item_57/58
776	   *  随机、音量 ×0.5×style×0.05（style 6-10 → 0.15-0.25）、音高扰动 ±0.4
777	   *  由双素材近似）；≥5 消亡；全速翻面（与 lastVelocity 逐轴对比后取负）。 */
778	  private meowBounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
779	    const st = game.world.store;
780	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
781	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
782	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
783	    };
784	    this.meowCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, this.meowCount + 1));   // Clamp(:16796)
785	    const style = 5 + this.meowCount;
786	    game.playSfxFiles([Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'Item_57' : 'Item_58'],
787	      0.5 * style * 0.05, this.cx, this.cy);
788	    if (this.meowCount >= 5) { this.killNow(game); return true; }
789	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
790	      this.x -= this.vx;
791	      this.vx = -this.vx;
792	    }
793	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
794	      this.y -= this.vy;
795	      this.vy = -this.vy;
796	    }
797	    return true;
798	  }
799	
800	  // ================= 食人鱼 190（aiStyle 39，Projectile.cs:26065-26315）=================
801	  // 状态机 1:1：ai[0]=0 直飞咬敌（:26245）/ 1 返回玩家（:26266）；ai[1]=0 未咬可咬 /
802	  // >0 咬住（Damage_PVE :12447 首咬写入 index+1）/ −1 松手禁再咬（:26095）。
803	  // 咬住伤害 = 普通 hitbox 接触走 Damage_PVE（:11868-11871 localNPCImmunity 门 +
804	  // :11905 全局 immune 门），localNPCHitCooldown=14t 同敌周期撕咬（:13157）。
805	
806	  /** 姿态记录（:26119-26125/:26139-26143/:26252-26287 三处 rotation 赋值：
807	   *  vx<0 → spriteDirection=−1 + rotation=atan2(−vy,−vx)，等效 draw 的
808	   *  scale(−1)+rotate(π−ang) 变换——此处只存未镜像角 + 翻转位） */
809	  private pFace(vx: number, vy: number): void {
810	    this.pRot = Math.atan2(vy, vx);
811	    this.pFlip = vx < 0;
812	  }
813	
814	  /** 统一消亡出口 + 回收补弹（Kill 语义；在场数 <3 且仍按住 → 补满 3，
815	   *  Player.cs:42856-42868 ItemCheck num∈(0,3) 置 flag4 → :47768-47786 生成 3−num 条） */
816	  private piranhaKill(game: GameHooks): void {
817	    this.dead = true;
818	    game.onProjectileDeath?.(this.projId, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
819	    if (this.piranha?.channel()) this.pTopUp(game);
820	  }
821	
822	  /** 回收补弹（ItemCheck_Shoot 1156 专支 Player.cs:47768-47786：生成
823	   *  3−在场数 条；速度分量各 +Next(−40,41)×0.05 扰动；出生点=玩家枪口。
824	   *  无重播 UseSound——原版 channel 期 itemTime 恒被 SetDummyItemTime(5) 顶住，
825	   *  无第二次 use 起手声） */
826	  private pTopUp(game: GameHooks): void {
827	    const p = game.player as { cx: number; cy: number } | null;
828	    const ents = (game as unknown as {
829	      entities?: { projectiles?: unknown[]; add?: (e: unknown, b: string) => void };
830	    }).entities;
831	    if (!p || !ents?.add) return;
832	    const alive = (ents.projectiles ?? []).filter(
833	      (o) => (o as { projId?: number }).projId === PIRANHA_PROJ && !(o as { dead?: boolean }).dead).length;
834	    const aim = this.piranha!.aim();
835	    const a = Math.atan2(aim.y - p.cy, aim.x - p.cx);
836	    for (let i = 0; i < 3 - alive; i++) {
837	      const np = new Arrow(p.cx + Math.cos(a) * 14, p.cy - 4 + Math.sin(a) * 14,
838	        Math.cos(a) * this.pShootSpeed + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,
839	        Math.sin(a) * this.pShootSpeed + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) - 40) * 0.05,
840	        this.damage, this.knockback, PIRANHA_PROJ, null,
841	        { grav: 0, life: PIRANHA_LIFE, piranha: this.piranha! });
842	      np.critChance = this.critChance;
843	      np.critBonus = this.critBonus;
844	      np.armorPen = this.armorPen;
845	      np.frostEligible = this.frostEligible;
846	      np.pShootSpeed = this.pShootSpeed;
847	      ents.add(np, 'projectiles');
848	    }
849	  }
850	
851	  private piranhaStep(game: GameHooks): void {
852	    // Update :15329 DecrementLocalImmuneTimeCounters——localNPCImmunity 逐帧递减
853	    for (const [k, v] of this.pHitCd) {
854	      if (v <= 1) this.pHitCd.delete(k); else this.pHitCd.set(k, v - 1);
855	    }
856	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }
857	    this.age++;
858	    // 淡入（:26067-26071）
859	    this.pAlpha = Math.max(0, this.pAlpha - 50);
860	    const p = game.player as { cx: number; cy: number; vx?: number; vy?: number; dead?: boolean; facing?: number } | null;
861	    // :26072-26077 玩家失活/死亡/离主 >2000 → Kill
862	    if (!p || p.dead) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }
863	    const dxp = p.cx - this.cx, dyp = p.cy - this.cy;
864	    const dp = Math.hypot(dxp, dyp);
865	    if (dp > 2000) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }
866	    // :26078-26087 淡入完成后每帧面向食人鱼（SetDummyItemTime 持物锁未接，登记 GAP）
867	    if (this.pAlpha === 0 && p.facing !== undefined) p.facing = this.cx > p.cx ? 1 : -1;
868	    // :26093-26096 松手（淡入完成后才生效）：返回 + ai[1]=−1 禁再咬
869	    if (this.pAlpha === 0 && !this.piranha!.channel()) {
870	      this.pAi0 = 1; this.pAi1 = -1; this.pTarget = null;
871	    }
872	    // :26098-26102 咬住超距（离主 >1500）脱咬转返回（ai[1]=0 仍可再咬）
873	    if (this.pAi1 > 0 && dp > 1500) { this.pAi1 = 0; this.pAi0 = 1; this.pTarget = null; }
874	    let tileCollide = true;
875	    if (this.pAi1 > 0) {
876	      tileCollide = false;                                   // :26105
877	      const t = this.pTarget;
878	      if (t && !t.dead && t.hp > 0) {                        // :26107 目标存活
879	        const speed = 16;                                    // :26109 num313 咬敌速
880	        const dx = t.cx - this.cx, dy = t.cy - this.cy;
881	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
882	        if (d < speed) {
883	          this.vx = dx; this.vy = dy;                        // :26116-26117 残差直抵
884	          if (d > speed / 3) {
885	            this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                    // :26119-26125 朝目标
886	            this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;                        // :26126-26129 type 190 咬定
887	          }
888	        } else {
889	          const dd = d === 0 ? 0.0001 : d;                   // :26132-26136
890	          this.vx = dx / dd * speed; this.vy = dy / dd * speed;
891	          this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                      // :26138-26143
892	        }
893	        this.x += t.vx; this.y += t.vy;                      // :26159-26161 跟随目标速度
894	        // :26162-26178 同主多弹排斥（<8px 逐轴 ∓4——多鱼咬同一大体型怪时散开）
895	        const sibs = (game as unknown as {
896	          entities?: { projectiles?: Array<{ projId?: number; dead?: boolean; x?: number; y?: number; cx?: number; cy?: number }> };
897	        }).entities?.projectiles;
898	        if (sibs) {
899	          for (const o of sibs) {
900	            if (o === this || o.projId !== PIRANHA_PROJ || o.dead) continue;
901	            if (Math.hypot((o.cx ?? 0) - this.cx, (o.cy ?? 0) - this.cy) >= 8) continue;
902	            if (this.x < (o.x ?? 0)) this.vx -= 4; else this.vx += 4;
903	            if (this.y < (o.y ?? 0)) this.vy -= 4; else this.vy += 4;
904	          }
905	        }
906	        this.pAi0 = 1;                                       // :26184-26188 咬住期恒置 1
907	      } else {
908	        // :26190-26196 目标死亡 → 清咬
909	        this.pAi1 = 0; this.pTarget = null;
910	        // :26197-26223 半径 3000 内视线可达最近敌（CanBeChasedBy :91070 近似 =
911	        //  存活/未无敌/可受伤；Manhattan 度量 num322）
912	        let best: PiranhaTarget | null = null;
913	        let bestScore = 3000;                                // :26205 num319
914	        let bx = 0, by = 0;
915	        for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
916	          const e = ent as unknown as PiranhaTarget;
917	          if (e.dead || e.hp <= 0 || e.dontTakeDamage) continue;
918	          const score = Math.abs(this.cx - e.cx) + Math.abs(this.cy - e.cy);
919	          if (score >= bestScore) continue;
920	          if (!canHit(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e.x, e.y, e.w, e.h)) continue;
921	          bestScore = score; best = e; bx = e.cx; by = e.cy;
922	        }
923	        if (best) {
924	          // :26223-26241 转咬新目标（立即入咬住态，速度 16 直指）
925	          const dd = Math.hypot(bx - this.cx, by - this.cy) || 0.0001;
926	          this.vx = (bx - this.cx) / dd * 16;
927	          this.vy = (by - this.cy) / dd * 16;
928	          this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);
929	          this.pAi0 = 0; this.pAi1 = 1; this.pTarget = best;
930	        }
931	        // 未找到新目标 → pAi0 已被 :26188 段置 1 → 返回（即便仍按住）
932	      }
933	    } else if (this.pAi0 === 0) {
934	      // :26245-26261 直飞：无追踪（首咬靠接触），离主 >700 → 返回
935	      if (dp > 700) this.pAi0 = 1;                           // :26247-26251
936	      this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                          // :26252-26261
937	    } else {
938	      // :26266-26299 返回：穿墙、速度 20 直指玩家、<70px 回收
939	      tileCollide = false;
940	      this.pFace(this.vx, this.vy);                          // :26267-26287（保留来向姿态）
941	      if (dp < 70) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }       // :26276-26279
942	      const dd = dp === 0 ? 0.0001 : dp;                     // :26273 num324=20
943	      this.vx = dxp / dd * 20;
944	      this.vy = dyp / dd * 20;
945	      this.x += p.vx ?? 0; this.y += p.vy ?? 0;              // :26296-26299 type 190
946	    }
947	    // :26301-26310 帧推进（4t/帧 4 帧循环；AI 侧真源，draw 消费 pFrame）
948	    if (++this.pFrameCounter >= 4) {
949	      this.pFrameCounter = 0;
950	      this.pFrame = (this.pFrame + 1) % 4;
951	    }
952	    // 位移积分（Update 运动段在 AI 后）
953	    this.x += this.vx;
954	    this.y += this.vy;
955	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
956	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
957	    if (!game.world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.piranhaKill(game); return; }
958	    // 撞块（HandleMovement :17940-17964 aiStyle 39 档：HitTiles 尘 + dig 声 +
959	    //  ai[0]=1 返回，弹体不消亡；咬住/返回期 tileCollide=false 整段跳过）
960	    const tileType = game.world.store.get(tx, ty);
961	    if (tileCollide && tileType !== 0) {
962	      game.cutTile(tx, ty);
963	      if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
964	        this.x -= this.vx; this.y -= this.vy;                // 碰撞回退（停在墙前）
965	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
966	        this.pAi0 = 1;                                       // :17958 返回
967	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#B0A080', 4, 1.5, { life: 18 });
968	        game.playSfx('dig', 1, this.cx, this.cy);            // :17962 SoundID.Dig(0)
969	      }
970	    }
971	    // 小动物接触（Damage_PVE 对 NPC 通用：小动物 friendly=false 亦受击；
972	    //  penetrate=−1 弹体不消耗；非 CanBeChasedBy 目标不会成为咬住对象）
973	    hitCritters(this, game);
974	    // 命中结算（Damage_PVE :11850 迭代 + Damage_PVE_Inner :11888）

 *  玩家 min(16, 距离) 直飞，贴身 <20px 时 AddBuff(145 MoonLeech, 840t/专家 960t)
 *  （:30722-30732，本仓经典模式恒 840t = 14s）；age≥330 或玩家失联 → ai[0] 翻负
 *  回程段：朝 额前(0,+216) 直飞，<20px Kill（吸血视觉闭环）。
 *  弹体自旋角 = (源心 - 玩家心 + (0,216)).ToRotation() + π/2（:30717）。 */
export class MoonLeechProj extends Entity {
  w = 16; h = 16;
  vx = 0; vy = 0;
  private srcId: number;
  private out = true;      // ai[0] 符号：true = 出击 / false = 回程
  private age = 0;         // localAI[0]
  private fed = false;     // localAI[1]（已吸血，不重复授 buff）
  private life = 600;      // timeLeft
  dead = false;

  constructor(src: Enemy, player: Player) {
    super();
    this.srcId = src.id;
    this.x = src.cx - this.w / 2;
    this.y = src.cy + 216 - this.h / 2;   // 额前 (0,+216)
    const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    this.vx = (dx / d) * 6; this.vy = (dy / d) * 6;   // :37823 出速 6（AI 段接管为 16 上限）
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    const src = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.srcId);
    const player = game.player;
    if (--this.life <= 0 || !src || src.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }
    if (!player || player.dead) { if (this.out) this.out = false; }   // :30706-30712 玩家失联即回程
    this.age++;
    if (this.age >= 330 && this.out) this.out = false;                // :30702-30705
    // 出击：朝玩家心直飞（:30722-30725）；回程：朝源额前 (0,+216)（:30734-30738）
    const tx = this.out ? player.cx : src.cx;
    const ty = this.out ? player.cy : src.cy + 216;
    const dx = tx - this.cx, dy = ty - this.cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    const spd = Math.min(16, d);
    if (d > 0.001) { this.vx = (dx / d) * spd; this.vy = (dy / d) * spd; }
    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
    // 贴身结算：出击段 <20px 授 MoonLeech 145（:30726-30732）；回程段 <20px Kill（:30740-30743）
    if (this.out && d < 20 && !this.fed) {
      this.fed = true;
      player.buffs.apply(BuffType.MoonLeech, 840 / 60);
    } else if (!this.out && d < 20) {
      this.dead = true;
    }
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const img = projSprite(456);
    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
    // 自旋（:30717）：(源心 - 玩家心 + (0,216)) 角 + π/2——回程矢量已同向，直接用速度角近似
    const ang = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.rotate(ang);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/** 月噬怪召出(:37828-37842 残肢 / :37975-37989 月噬攻击):头在 lt/inSlot=120/180/240
 *  于玩家处召 NPC 401,ai[0]=头 whoAmI+1 / ai[1]=源弹 456 索引。
 *  原版门禁 = 遍历全部 456 弹,其 ai[1] 指向的玩家 FindBuffIndex(145) != -1 才召
 *  （每弹一只）。本仓单玩家:debuff 145 已由 MoonLeechProj 贴身授予 → 玩家身中
 *  MoonLeech 即召,等价替换旧「弹已射出即命中」无条件近似
 *  (ai[1] 源弹索引以 -1 代,moonLeechAI 以出生点代弹着点,不受影响)。 */
function spawnMoonLeech(game: GameHooks, head: Enemy, player: Player): void {
  if (!player.buffs.moonLeech) return;
  const leech = spawnPart(game, head, 401);
  if (!leech) return;
  leech.x = player.cx - leech.w / 2;
  leech.y = player.cy - leech.h / 2;
  leech.ai0 = head.id + 1;
  leech.ai1 = -1;
  leech.ai2 = 0;
  leech.vx = 0; leech.vy = 0;
}

    this.damage = dmg;

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    const st = game.world.store;
    // 接触熔断（Projectile.Damage :12330-12340:碰玩家/NPC → timeLeft 钳 3）
    const pl = game.player;
    if (pl && !pl.dead
      && this.x < pl.x + pl.w && this.x + this.w > pl.x
      && this.y < pl.y + pl.h && this.y + this.h > pl.y && this.fuse > 3) this.fuse = 3;
    if (--this.fuse <= 0) { this.explode(game); return; }
    this.rot += this.vx * 0.1;             // 滚动（AI_016 :44909-44915）
    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.2, 16);
    let nx = this.x + this.vx, ny = this.y + this.vy;
    const solidAtP = (px: number, py: number): boolean => {
      const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE), ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
      return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
    };
    if (solidAtP(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2)
      || solidAtP(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h - 1)) {
      this.vx *= -0.4; nx = this.x;        // 撞墙反弹（:18344-18350）
    }
    if (solidAtP(nx + this.w / 2, ny + this.h - 1)) {
      if (this.vy > 0.7) this.vy *= -0.4;  // 落地反弹（:18351-18357）
      else { this.vy = 0; this.vx *= 0.97; }
      ny = this.y;
    }
    this.x = nx; this.y = ny;
    if (!st.inBounds(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))) this.dead = true;
  }
  /** Kill :68638-68696：80² 爆盒 + 尘爆 + 音效 */
  private explode(game: GameHooks): void {
    this.dead = true;
    game.onProjectileDeath?.(681, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
    game.playSfx('explosion', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FFD24A', 14, 4, { life: 30, grav: -0.05 });
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF9A3C', 10, 3, { life: 40 });
    const bx = this.cx - 40, by = this.cy - 40;   // 80×80（:68689-68695）
    const pl = game.player;
    if (pl && !pl.dead
      && pl.x < bx + 80 && pl.x + pl.w > bx && pl.y < by + 80 && pl.y + pl.h > by) {
      game.damagePlayer(this.damage, this.cx, this.cy);
    }
    for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
      // 永恒水晶 548 吃敌对爆伤（DD2 敌怪对水晶的伤害通道）
      if (n.dead || n.vanillaId !== 548 || n.dontTakeDamage) continue;
      if (n.x < bx + 80 && n.x + n.w > bx && n.y < by + 80 && n.y + n.h > by) {
        n.hurt(this.damage, 0, 0, game);
      }
    }
  }
  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_681.png');
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.rotate(this.rot);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    if (img) ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
    else { ctx.fillStyle = '#3A3A3A'; ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h); }
    ctx.restore();
    void cam;
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 683 食人魔跺地波（ProjectileID.cs:1726;SetDefaults :7003+ aiStyle 135/30²/
//   tileCollide=false/ignoreWater）。AI_135_OgreStomp（Projectile.cs:65725-65830）：
//   原地 9t,盒从 16×5=80² 扩到 16×40=640²,每 3t 一波尘爆;伤害走 hostile 盒。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export class DD2OgreSmash extends Entity {
  w = 80; h = 80;
  dead = false;
  private ai0 = 0;
  private dmg: number;
  private readonly hitIds = new Set<number>();
  constructor(x: number, y: number, dmg: number) {
    super();
    this.x = x - this.w / 2; this.y = y - this.h / 2;
    this.dmg = dmg;
  }
  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    this.ai0 += 1;
    if (this.ai0 > 9) { this.dead = true; game.onProjectileDeath?.(683, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h); return; }
    // 扩盒（:65740 Size = 16×lerp(5,40,ai0/9)）
    const size = Math.round(16 * lerp(5, 40, this.ai0 / 9));
    const ncx = this.cx, ncy = this.cy;    // base.Center 保持（:65738-65741 先取心再扩盒）
    this.w = size; this.h = size;
    this.x = ncx - size / 2; this.y = ncy - size / 2;
    // 伤害（hostile 盒:玩家 + 永恒水晶,同弹 id 免疫帧防连击）
    const pl = game.player;
    if (pl && !pl.dead
      && pl.x < this.x + this.w && pl.x + pl.w > this.x
      && pl.y < this.y + this.h && pl.y + pl.h > this.y) {
      game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy);
    }
    for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
      if (n.dead || n.vanillaId !== 548 || n.dontTakeDamage) continue;
      if (this.hitIds.has(n.id)) continue;
      if (n.x < this.x + this.w && n.x + n.w > this.x
        && n.y < this.y + this.h && n.y + n.h > this.y) {
        this.hitIds.add(n.id);
        n.hurt(this.dmg, 0, 0, game);
      }
    }
    if (this.ai0 % 3 === 0) {              // 每 3t 尘爆（:65746-65750）
      game.spawnParticles(this.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.w,
        this.y + this.h - 8, '#B09060', 4, 2.4, { life: 30 });
    }
  }
  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.8;
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_683.png');
    const s = this.w / 30;                 // 贴图原尺寸 30²,按盒缩放
    if (img) ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, 30 * s, 30 * s);
    else { ctx.fillStyle = '#8A6A4A'; ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h); }
    ctx.restore();
    void cam;
  }
}

```

// 原版投射物数据（tools/extract-projectiles.mjs 从 Terarria1456 Projectile.cs SetDefaults 提取）。
// 注意 1456 的 SetDefaults 是 if/else-if 链（非 switch），且长矛/悠悠球/连枷等族走
// DefaultTo*() 辅助方法（aiStyle 19/99/15）、悠悠球批量定义在 `type >= A && type <= B` 区间块。
import projJson from './vanilla-projectiles.json';

export interface ProjectileData {
  aiStyle?: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  penetrate?: number;
  timeLeft?: number;
  damage?: number;
  light?: number;
  /** 绘制 scale（SetDefaults，196 款非 1；绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生 × scale，非判定盒宽） */
  scale?: number;
  /** 每帧额外子步（SetDefaults，249 款非 0；总步数 = 1+extraUpdates——子弹 14=1、高速弹 207=2） */
  extraUpdates?: number;
  friendly?: boolean;
  melee?: boolean;
  ranged?: boolean;
  magic?: boolean;
  minion?: boolean;
  hostile?: boolean;
  tileCollide?: boolean;
  ignoreWater?: boolean;
}

const TABLE = projJson as unknown as Record<string, ProjectileData>;

/** 原版 projectile type → 投射物数据（无条目返回 null） */
export function projectileData(type: number): ProjectileData | null {
  return TABLE[String(type)] ?? null;
}

// ---- aiStyle 家族常量（ProjectileAIStyleIDs / 源码辅助方法实测值） ----
export const AI_ARROW = 1;        // 直飞弹（箭/子弹/大部分魔法弹）
export const AI_THROWN = 2;       // 抛物线投掷物（手里剑/飞刀，重力 0.3/tick）
export const AI_BOOMERANG = 3;    // 回旋镖（飞出后返回）
export const AI_FLAIL = 15;       // 连枷（抛掷甩链）
export const AI_GRENADE = 16;     // 弹跳爆炸物（手雷/炸弹/炸药，引信爆炸）
export const AI_SPEAR = 19;       // 长矛（随角色前刺）
export const AI_ANCHORED = 13;    // 锚定链族（AI_013:鱼叉/Golem拳/KO加农/链刀——锚在角色上伸缩,归长矛近似）
export const AI_YOYO = 99;        // 悠悠球（按住持续滞留）
export const AI_SHORTSWORD = 161; // 短剑直刺
export const AI_WHIP = 165;       // 鞭
export const AI_GLOWSTICK = 14;   // 荧光棒族（弹跳物理/粘性/妖精）

  /** 发射(:54903/:55175/:55325 三族瞄准公式统一在此) */
  private shootAt(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks, params: TownShotParams, range: number): void {
    const st = world.store;
    let proj = params.proj;
    let dmg = params.dmg;
    let speed = params.speed;
    let kb = params.kb;
    let spread = params.spread;
    // 229 PrettySafe 近距保命换炮弹(:55128-55135)
    const pretty = TOWN_PRETTY_SAFE[this.vanillaId];
    const target = this.pickSideTarget(world, game, range);
    if (params.panic && pretty && target && Math.hypot(target.cx - this.cx, target.cy - this.cy) < pretty
      && this.atkTick === params.shootTick) {
      proj = params.panic.proj; dmg = params.panic.dmg; speed = params.panic.speed;
      kb = params.panic.kb; spread = params.panic.spread;
    }
    // 209 三选一弹(:55139)
    if (this.vanillaId === 209 && !params.panic) {
      const pick = [134, 133, 135][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)];
      proj = pick;
      const m: Record<number, [number, number, number, number, number]> = {
        134: [20, 13, 4, 0.1, 20], 133: [25, 10, 6, 0.2, 10], 135: [30, 12, 7, 0.2, 30],
      };
      [dmg, speed, kb, spread] = [m[pick][0], m[pick][1], m[pick][2], m[pick][3]];
    }
    // 瞄准:弓 = 目标上抬 aimLift 取向;近战/魔法 = aimLiftScaled×(dist/range 归一)上抬
    let vx = this.facing, vy = 0;
    if (target) {
      let lift = params.aimLift ?? 0;
      if (this.atkState === 10 || this.atkState === 14) {
        const dist = Math.hypot(target.cx - this.cx, target.cy - this.cy);
        lift = (params.aimLiftScaled ?? 0) * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, dist / range));
        vy = -1; // 近战/魔法默认上抛分量(初值 -UnitY)
      }
      const dx = target.cx - this.cx;
      const dy = target.cy - lift - this.cy;
      const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      vx = dx / dl; vy = dy / dl;
      if (Math.sign(vx) !== this.facing) { vx = this.facing; vy = this.atkState === 12 ? 0 : -1; }
    } else if (this.atkState !== 12) {
      vy = -1;
    }
    vx *= speed; vy *= speed;
    // 散布(Utils.RandomVector2 ±spread;近战态恒 0)
    if (spread > 0) {
      vx += (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * spread;
      vy += (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * spread;
    }
    const dmgFinal = Math.round(dmg * townNpcDamageMult(0));
    const sx = this.cx + this.facing * 16 - 5;
    const sy = this.cy - 2 - 5;
    // 落点弹(160/663):目标区域生成 velocity=0;实心重掷近似 ≤5 次
    if (speed === 0 && target) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
        const px = target.cx - 16 + Math.random() * 32;
        const py = target.cy - 16 + Math.random() * 32;
        if (!st.isSolid(Math.floor(px / TILE), Math.floor(py / TILE))) {
          game.spawnTownShot?.(new TownShot(px - 5, py - 5, 0, 0, dmgFinal, kb, proj, { noGravity: true }));
          return;
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    game.spawnTownShot?.(new TownShot(sx, sy, vx, vy, dmgFinal, kb, proj, {
      life: this.vanillaId === 588 ? 480 : undefined,
    }));
  }

481	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
482	export class InsanityShadowProj extends Entity {
483	  w = 40; h = 40;
484	  dead = false;
485	  life = 480;
486	  alpha = 255;              // 255=全透明（SetDefaults）→ 淡入至 50
487	  private ai0: number;
488	  private readonly ai1: number;
489	  private rotation = 0;
490	  constructor(target: { cx: number; cy: number; vx: number } | null, private dmg: number, hint: number) {
491	    super();
492	    // RandomizeInsanityShadowFor 单人近似：玩家环上随机点 + 段直选
493	    const c = target ?? { cx: 0, cy: 0, vx: 0 };
494	    const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
495	    this.x = c.cx - Math.cos(a) * 200 - this.w / 2;
496	    this.y = c.cy - Math.sin(a) * 200 - this.h / 2;
497	    this.vx = Math.cos(a) * 4;
498	    this.vy = Math.sin(a) * 4;
499	    this.ai0 = [0, 180, 300, 390][hint % 4];
500	    this.ai1 = a;
501	  }
502	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
503	    this.ai0 += 1;
504	    // 变体段（:39903-39940）
505	    let variation = 0, start = 0, len = 180;
506	    if (this.ai0 >= 390) { variation = 3; start = 390; len = 90; }
507	    else if (this.ai0 >= 300) { variation = 2; start = 300; len = 90; }
508	    else if (this.ai0 >= 180) { variation = 1; start = 180; len = 120; }
509	    const fc = this.ai0 - start;
510	    // :39825-39841 alpha：段尾 15t 淡出至 255,否则淡入至 50
511	    if (fc > len - 15) this.alpha = Math.min(255, this.alpha + 25);
512	    else this.alpha = Math.max(50, this.alpha - 25);
513	    if (fc >= len - 1 || --this.life <= 0) {
514	      this.dead = true;
515	      game.onProjectileDeath?.(965, this.x, this.y);
516	      return;
517	    }
518	    if (variation === 0) {                       // :39847-39856 减速滑行
519	      this.vx *= 0.98; this.vy *= 0.98;
520	      this.rotation = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (this.vx < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
521	    } else if (variation === 1) {                // :39857-39874 绕轴摆荡
522	      const t = fc / len;
523	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 0.1) { this.vx *= 0.95; this.vy *= 0.95; }
524	      const pivotD = 70 * (this.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
525	      const a1 = remap(t, 0.3, 0.5, 0, 1) * remap(t, 0.45, 0.5, 1, 0);
526	      const a2 = remap(t, 0.5, 0.55, 0, 1) * remap(t, 0.5, 1, 1, 0);
527	      this.rotation += (a1 * (Math.PI / 60) + a2 * (-Math.PI * 8 / 60)) * -(this.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
528	      const px = this.cx - Math.cos(this.rotation) * pivotD;
529	      const py = this.cy - Math.sin(this.rotation) * pivotD;
530	      this.x = px + Math.cos(this.rotation) * pivotD - this.w / 2;
531	      this.y = py + Math.sin(this.rotation) * pivotD - this.h / 2;
532	    } else if (variation === 2) {                // :39875-39888 俯冲（0.4 处峰值速）
533	      const t = fc / len;
534	      const v1 = remap(t, 0, 0.4, 1, 0);
535	      const v2 = remap(t, 0.3, 0.4, 0, 1) * remap(t, 0.4, 1, 1, 0);
536	      const spd = v1 * 2 + v2 * 8 + 0.01;
537	      this.vx = Math.cos(this.ai1) * spd;
538	      this.vy = Math.sin(this.ai1) * spd;
539	      this.rotation = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (this.vx < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
540	    } else {                                     // :39889-39899 旋进
541	      const w = this.ai1;
542	      const cos = Math.cos(w), sin = Math.sin(w);
543	      const nvx = this.vx * cos - this.vy * sin;
544	      const nvy = this.vx * sin + this.vy * cos;
545	      this.vx = nvx; this.vy = nvy;
546	      this.rotation = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (this.vx < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
547	    }
548	    const p = game.player;
549	    if (p && !p.dead && this.alpha < 250
550	      && p.x < this.x + this.w && p.x + p.w > this.x && p.y < this.y + this.h && p.y + p.h > this.y) {
551	      game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy);
552	    }
553	  }
554	  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, _cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {
555	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
556	    if (!ctx) return;
557	    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_965.png');
558	    ctx.save();
559	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - this.alpha / 255;
560	    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
561	    ctx.rotate(this.rotation);
562	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
563	    if (img) ctx.drawImage(img, -20, -20, 40, 40);
564	    else { ctx.fillStyle = '#6020B0'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 18, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); }
565	    ctx.restore();
566	  }
567	}
568	
569	function remap(x: number, a: number, b: number, c: number, d: number): number {
570	  if (x < a) return c;
571	  if (x > b) return d;
572	  return c + (d - c) * ((x - a) / (b - a));
573	}
574	
575	/** 弹幕入桶（与 duke 系同款：hostile 类自管运动,dead 由桶清理） */
576	function addProj(game: GameHooks, p: Entity): void {
577	  (game as unknown as { entities: { add(e: Entity, bucket: string): void } })
578	    .entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
579	}

  }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 环绕球投射物 539/574(Projectile.cs:5864-5878 SetDefaults + AI_102 :32040-32280)
//  539(水流入侵者 407 甩出):绕父 407 盘旋 210t(0.15/0.075 符号步进钳 6,随父位移),
//    到点取最近玩家方向(±50px 抖动 ×0.8-1.2)以 16 速直冲;亡时(撞块/到期)80×80
//    范围 60 伤(:69366-69405)。
//  574(悬浮脑 420 环绕):随 420 存活;每 180t 且对玩家 CanHitLine → 线发 576 弹
//    (8 速 65 伤)后重置计时继续盘旋;悬浮脑瞬移时全量计时 -30(accelerate)。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export class LunarOrb extends Entity {
  w = 18; h = 18;
  vx: number; vy: number;
  readonly kind: 539 | 574;
  readonly parentId: number;
  /** v6 联机：父体 netId（访客端父=msg23 傀儡；0=本端真身走 parentId 查找） */
  parentNetId = 0;
  private ai0 = 0;
  private dmg: number;
  private life = 600;
  private frame = 0;
  private frameT = 0;
  dead = false;

  constructor(parent: Enemy | null, kind: 539 | 574, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, x?: number, y?: number) {
    super();
    this.kind = kind;
    this.parentId = parent?.id ?? 0;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.dmg = dmg;
    if (kind === 539) { this.w = 18; this.h = 30; }
    // v6 傀儡重建：parent=null 时由调用方传显式坐标
    this.x = (x ?? parent!.cx) - this.w / 2;
    this.y = (y ?? parent!.cy) - this.h / 2;
  }

  /** v6 联机打包：kind=8，tag=`${539|574},${parentId}`。
   *  访客重建后本地跑盘旋/冲刺/爆炸（确定性自包含，父=傀儡）；
   *  574 的线发 576 弹经 !netPuppet 门禁排除（房主侧已发） */
  netProjMeta(): NetProjMeta {
    return { kind: 8, tag: `${this.kind},${this.parentId}`, aux: 0, dmg: this.dmg };
  }

  /** 悬浮脑瞬移同步:全场计时 -30(:41682-41691) */
  delay(t: number): void { this.ai0 -= t; }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    // v6 联机：傀儡端按 netId 找父（msg23 傀儡）；本端真身按本地 id
    const parent = this.parentNetId
      ? (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.netId === this.parentNetId)
      : (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.parentId);
    const orbitT = this.kind === 539 ? 210 : 180;
    const alive = !!parent && !parent.dead;
    if (this.kind === 574 && !alive) { this.dead = true; return; }   // :32083-32092
    this.ai0 += 1;
    if (this.life-- <= 0) { this.explode(game); return; }
    if (this.ai0 < orbitT && alive) {
      // 盘旋:随父位移 + 符号步进逼近父心(钳 6)(:32100-32118)
      this.x += parent!.x - parent!.prevX;
      this.y += parent!.y - parent!.prevY;
      this.vx += Math.sign(parent!.cx - this.cx) * 0.15;
      this.vy += Math.sign(parent!.cy - this.cy) * 0.075;
      const v = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
      if (v > 6) { this.vx = (this.vx / v) * 6; this.vy = (this.vy / v) * 6; }
      if (this.kind === 539 && ++this.frameT >= 4) { this.frameT = 0; this.frame++; }
    } else if (this.ai0 === orbitT) {
      const p = game.player;
      if (this.kind === 539) {
        // 直冲最近玩家(:32185-32212):方向 ±50px 抖动 ×0.8-1.2,16 速
        const tx = p.cx + (Math.random() * 100 - 50), ty = p.cy + (Math.random() * 100 - 50);
        const kx = 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4, ky = 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4;
        const dx = (tx - this.cx) * kx, dy = (ty - this.cy) * ky;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
        this.vx = (dx / d) * 16; this.vy = (dy / d) * 16;
      } else {
        // 线发 576 弹(:32204-32211):CanHitLine 门,8 速 65 伤;发射后重置计时。
        // v6 傀儡不本地线发——房主的 576 弹经 msg27 同步,双端各发会翻倍
        if (!this.netPuppet && !p.dead && canHitLine(game.world.store, this.cx, this.cy, 0, 0, p.cx, p.cy, 0, 0)) {
          const dx = p.cx - this.cx, dy = p.cy - this.cy;
          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
          (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (x: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(
            new Arrow(this.cx, this.cy, (dx / d) * 8, (dy / d) * 8, 65, 0, 576, null,
              { grav: 0, life: 600, pierce: 1, hostile: true }), 'projectiles');
        }
        this.ai0 = 0;
      }
    }
    if (this.kind === 539 && this.ai0 > orbitT && ++this.frameT >= 2) {
      this.frameT = 0; this.frame++;                  // 冲刺段帧率加倍(:32227-32235)
    }
    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;             // 539 tileCollide / 574 穿墙
    if (this.kind === 539) {
      const st = game.world.store;
      const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.explode(game); return; }
    }
  }

  /** 539 亡时 80×80 范围伤害 + 尘爆(:69366-69405) */
  private explode(game: GameHooks): void {
    this.dead = true;
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#8FE8FF', 20, 3.4, { life: 40, damp: 0.94 });
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#4FB8E8', 10, 2.2, { life: 36, damp: 0.94 });
    const p = game.player;
    if (p && !p.dead && this.dmg > 0) {
      const half = 40;                                // 80×80 居中
      if (p.x < this.cx + half && p.x + p.w > this.cx - half
        && p.y < this.cy + half && p.y + p.h > this.cy - half) {
        game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy, null);
      }
    }
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const img = projFrameImg(this.kind, this.kind === 574 ? 1 : this.frame);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.rotate(this.kind === 539 ? this.vx * 0.1 : Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    if (img) ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
    else {
      ctx.fillStyle = this.kind === 539 ? '#8FE8FF' : '#C060FF';
      ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

```

	{

			num = 30f;

		if (ai[0] > 9f)

			Kill();
			return;
		}
		velocity = Vector2.Zero;
		position = base.Center;
		base.Size = new Vector2(16f, 16f) * MathHelper.Lerp(5f, num, Utils.GetLerpValue(0f, 9f, ai[0]));
		base.Center = position;
		Point point = base.TopLeft.ToTileCoordinates();
		Point point2 = base.BottomRight.ToTileCoordinates();

function tryMakingSpike(
  game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, p: Player | null,
  srcX: number, srcY: number, dir: number, howMany: number, whichOne: number, xOffset: number,
): void {
  const DMG = 13;
  const x = srcX + xOffset * dir;
  const y = findBestSpikeY(game, e, p, srcY, x);
  if (!walkableAt(game, x, y)) return;
  const px = x * TILE + 8, py = y * TILE - 8;
  const ang = -Math.PI / 2 + whichOne * dir * 0.7 * (Math.PI / 4 / howMany);
  addProj(game, new DeerclopsSpikeProj(px, py, ang, DMG,
    0.1 + Math.random() * 0.1 + (xOffset * 1.1) / howMany));
}

/** AI_123_Deerclops_TryMakingSpike_FindBestY（:44900-44955）：目标侧向找最近可行走面,
 *  再上拔 20 格出实心、下探 20 格落到可行走 */
function findBestSpikeY(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, p: Player | null, srcY: number, x: number): number {
  void e;
  let y = srcY;
  if (p && !p.dead) {
    const tBottom = Math.floor((p.y + p.h) / TILE);
    const sgn = Math.sign(tBottom - srcY) || 1;
    const end = tBottom + sgn * 15;
    let best: number | null = null, bestD = Infinity;
    for (let i = srcY; i !== end; i += sgn) {
      if (!game.world.store.inBounds(x, i)) continue;
      if (walkableAt(game, x, i)) {
        const d = Math.abs(i * TILE + 8 - (p.y + p.h));
        if (best === null || d < bestD) { best = i; bestD = d; }
      }
    }
    if (best !== null) y = best;
  }
  for (let k = 0; k < 20; k++) {                 // 上拔出实心
    if (y < 10 || !solidAt(game, x, y)) break;
    y--;
  }
  for (let k = 0; k < 20; k++) {                 // 下探到可行走
    if (y > game.world.store.h - 10 || walkableAt(game, x, y)) break;
    y++;
  }
  return y;
}

}

```csharp
public static float GetLerpValue(float from, float to, float t, bool clamped = false)
{
    if (clamped)
    {
        if (from < to)
        {
            if (t < from) return 0;
            if (t > to) return 1;
        }
        else
        {
            if (t < to) return 1;   // hmm
            if (t > from) return 0;
        }
    }
    return (t - from) / (to - from);
}
```

For 539:
- Orbit phase (ai0 < 210): rotation = velocity.X * 0.1 (tilt wobble), frame advances every 4 ticks cycling 0..3.
- Dash phase (ai0 >= 210): rotation = AngleLerp(rotation, velocity.ToRotation() + π/2, 0.4) — i.e., converges to velocity angle + π/2; frame advances every 2 ticks.

The repo's LunarOrb: `ctx.rotate(this.kind === 539 ? this.vx * 0.1 : Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2)`. So 539 always uses vx*0.1 (orbit mode) even during dash, and 574 uses atan2+π/2 directly (no lerp — acceptable approximation). Frame: 539 advances every 4 (orbit) or 2 (dash) — repo does that (`frameT` 4 in orbit, 2 in dash) ✓. 574: frame fixed 1 ✓ (repo passes frame 1).

Now the draw SIZE: 539 native frame = 24×34, drawn at w×h = 18×30 → 0.75× and wrong aspect (0.6 vs 0.706). 574 native = 24×24, drawn at 18×18 → 0.75×. Both under the 2× threshold but aspect is off for 539. The task threshold: "原生尺寸×scale 与 w×h 差异显著,阈值>2×或纵横比翻转". 0.75× isn't >2×... but aspect mismatch 18:30 vs 24:34 — hmm "纵横比翻转" means aspect flip (wider↔taller), not ratio mismatch. 539 native is 24w×34h (taller), drawn 18×30 (taller) — no flip. Hmm. But the correct approach per the task's core principle is "原生×scale". Since these are already frame-sliced (projFrameImg) and close, but the principle says decouple from hitbox. 539: 24×34 vs 18×30 — the distortion is 25% linear. I'd fix it anyway since it's the same one-line change (native×scale instead of w×h) and it's unambiguously more correct. ```
export class CultistRitualCircle extends Entity {
  w = 14; h = 14;
  private srcId: number;
  private age = 0;         // ai[0]
  private alpha = 255;
  private rot = 0;
  private soundPlayed = false;  // localAI[1]
  dead = false;

  constructor(src: Enemy) {
    super();
    this.srcId = src.id;
    this.x = src.cx - this.w / 2;
    this.y = src.cy - this.h / 2;
  }

  /** (1-alpha/255)×0.6（:31208-31210） */
  private get scale(): number { return (1 - this.alpha / 255) * 0.6; }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    const src = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => n.id === this.srcId);
    // 锚消亡即 Kill（:31305-31320 flag44：ai[1] NPC 非活跃/非 439 族）
    if (!src || src.dead || src.vanillaId !== 439) { this.dead = true; return; }
    // 淡入/淡出（:31195-31210）：age<300 alpha-5/t，之后 +12/t
    this.alpha += this.age < 300 ? -5 : 12;
    this.alpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, this.alpha));
    this.rot += Math.PI / 210;                          // :31211
    if (!this.soundPlayed) {
      this.soundPlayed = true;
      // SoundID.Item123（Projectile.cs:31221，无坐标 = 全量不衰减）——原 roar 近似已销核
      game.playSfxFile?.('Item_123', 1);
    }
    // 完全显现期的收拢紫尘（:31218-31250，尘 228 / 半径 204×scale / 内圈 ×0.42）
    if (this.alpha === 0) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
        const inner = k === 1;
        const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const rr = 204 * this.scale * (inner ? 0.42 : 1);
        game.spawnParticles(this.cx + Math.cos(a) * rr, this.cy + Math.sin(a) * rr,
          '#A060E0', 1, inner ? 1.5 : 2, { life: 30 });
      }
    }
    this.age++;
    // :31282-31292 age==300 召 454/521——两 AI 族未移植（登记），跳过
    if (this.age >= 320) this.dead = true;              // :31294-31298
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const img = projSprite(490);
    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
    const s = this.scale;
    if (s <= 0.01) return;
    const iw = img.naturalWidth, ih = img.naturalHeight;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.rotate(this.rot);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    ctx.drawImage(img, -iw * s / 2, -ih * s / 2, iw * s, ih * s);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/** 克隆体生成出口(:65646-65650 NewNPC 440:X,Y 是底部中心坐标且 Y 再 +真身 height/2;
 *  ai[3]=真身 whoAmI / localAI[1]=仪式代数)。本仓:la3=真身引用、la1=代数、跳过出生显影 */
function spawnCultistClone(game: GameHooks, parent: Enemy, x: number, y: number, gen: number): Enemy | null {
  const c = spawnPart(game, parent, 440);
  if (!c) return null;
  const la = laOf(c);
  la.la3 = parent;
  la.la1 = gen;
  c.x = x - c.w / 2;
  c.y = y + parent.h / 2 - c.h;
  c.bInit = true; // 原版克隆体会落入 1t 的 -1 显影态(:65047),无实际效果,直接跳过
  return c;
}

/** 克隆体补射 468(:65375-65397 冰雾 / :65462-65499 火球第三拍 / :65531-65562 闪电 /
 *  :65796-65822 光第三拍):每只克隆体朝玩家(预判 20t)发 count 发,18 伤,
 *  (6+rand4) 速 ±spread 散布,出口 = 克隆体中心 + (facing*30, 12) */
function cloneSupplement(game: GameHooks, clones: Enemy[], player: Player, count: number, spread: number): void {
  for (const c of clones) {
    const dir = Math.sign(player.cx - c.cx);
    if (dir !== 0) c.facing = dir;
    const aim = aimLead(c, player, c.facing);
    for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
      const spd = 6 + Math.random() * 4;
      const [dvx, dvy] = rot(aim.x * spd, aim.y * spd, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * spread);
      shoot(game, c.cx + c.facing * 30, c.cy + 12, dvx, dvy, 18, 468);
    }
  }
}

  vy: number;

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number) {
    super();
    this.x = x - this.w / 2; this.y = y - this.h / 2;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.damage = dmg;
    this.fuse = 180;                       // timeLeft 缺省 180（aiStyle 16 无显式覆写）
  }
  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    const st = game.world.store;
    // 接触熔断（Projectile.Damage :12330-12340:碰玩家/NPC → timeLeft 钳 3）
    const pl = game.player;
    if (pl && !pl.dead
      && this.x < pl.x + pl.w && this.x + this.w > pl.x
      && this.y < pl.y + pl.h && this.y + this.h > pl.y && this.fuse > 3) this.fuse = 3;
    if (--this.fuse <= 0) { this.explode(game); return; }
    this.rot += this.vx * 0.1;             // 滚动（AI_016 :44909-44915）
    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.2, 16);
    let nx = this.x + this.vx, ny = this.y + this.vy;
    const solidAtP = (px: number, py: number): boolean => {
      const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE), ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
      return st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty);
    };
    if (solidAtP(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2)
      || solidAtP(nx + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h - 1)) {
      this.vx *= -0.4; nx = this.x;        // 撞墙反弹（:18344-18350）
    }
    if (solidAtP(nx + this.w / 2, ny + this.h - 1)) {
      if (this.vy > 0.7) this.vy *= -0.4;  // 落地反弹（:18351-18357）
      else { this.vy = 0; this.vx *= 0.97; }
      ny = this.y;
    }
    this.x = nx; this.y = ny;
    if (!st.inBounds(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))) this.dead = true;
  }
  /** Kill :68638-68696：80² 爆盒 + 尘爆 + 音效 */
  private explode(game: GameHooks): void {
    this.dead = true;
    game.onProjectileDeath?.(681, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
    game.playSfx('explosion', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FFD24A', 14, 4, { life: 30, grav: -0.05 });
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF9A3C', 10, 3, { life: 40 });
    const bx = this.cx - 40, by = this.cy - 40;   // 80×80（:68689-68695）
    const pl = game.player;
    if (pl && !pl.dead
      && pl.x < bx + 80 && pl.x + pl.w > bx && pl.y < by + 80 && pl.y + pl.h > by) {
      game.damagePlayer(this.damage, this.cx, this.cy);
    }
    for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
      // 永恒水晶 548 吃敌对爆伤（DD2 敌怪对水晶的伤害通道）
      if (n.dead || n.vanillaId !== 548 || n.dontTakeDamage) continue;
      if (n.x < bx + 80 && n.x + n.w > bx && n.y < by + 80 && n.y + n.h > by) {
        n.hurt(this.damage, 0, 0, game);
      }
    }
  }
  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_681.png');
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    ctx.rotate(this.rot);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    if (img) ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
    else { ctx.fillStyle = '#3A3A3A'; ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h); }
    ctx.restore();
    void cam;
  }
}

  54:  { proj: 585, dmg: 16, speed: 10, shootTick: 30, cdBase: 20, cdRand: 15, spread: 1, kb: 2 },

  160: { proj: 590, dmg: 40, speed: 0, shootTick: 15, burst: [30, 45], cdBase: 10, cdRand: 1, spread: 0, kb: 3,

  663: { proj: 950, dmg: 15, speed: 0, shootTick: 15, burst: [25, 35, 45], cdBase: 0, cdRand: 1, spread: 0, kb: 3,
