/** ai[0]==15 挥击态(无投射物;矩形挥击 :55406-55538) */
export const TOWN_SWING: Record<number, { dmg: number; kb: number; rect: number; cdBase: number; cdRand: number }> = {
  207: { dmg: 11, kb: 4.25, rect: 32, cdBase: 12, cdRand: 6 },

  353: { dmg: 10, kb: 5, rect: 32, cdBase: 15, cdRand: 8 },
};

/** GameDifficultyData.TownNPCDamageMultiplier(:69);本仓难度 = Classic 恒 1 */
export function townNpcDamageMult(difficulty: number): number {
  switch (difficulty) {
    case 1: return 1.5;   // Expert
    case 2: return 2;     // Legendary(含 Journey 2.0 同值)
    default: return 1;    // Classic / Journey(原版 Journey=2,此处 0 视经典)
  }
}

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T13:38:57.709Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
// 坐骑开火弹实体（Scutlix 眼激光 606 / Santank 炮击 930 / 子弹 14）
// ============================================================================

/** MountShot——坐骑能力弹（Projectile.NewProjectile 最小实体化，替代 TownShot 载体）：
 *  - extraUpdates 1:1（Projectile.extraUpdates+1 个子步/tick：606=3 子步 ×14 速 =
 *    42px/tick 与原版一致——TownShot 单步/tick 会使激光慢三倍）；
 *  - timeLeft 每tick 递减 1（子步不额外扣——原版 AI 多跑但 timeLeft 单调）；
 *  - 直线（noGravity）/ 抛物线（重力 0.3，Santank 炮击）两型；
 *  - 命中敌怪或实心 tile 即消散（penetrate 1 近似；930 穿透 -1 的弹跳/引信未实装——
 *    登记为命中消散近似） */
export class MountShot extends TownShot {
  /** 每 tick 子步数（extraUpdates + 1） */
  private substeps = 1;
  /** 重力加速度/tick（0 = 直线；TownShot 的 GRAV 常量不适用于 extraUpdates 子步） */
  gravity = 0.3;
  /** 落格标记（诊断用） */
  bounced = false;

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, knockback: number,
    projId: number, opts?: { noGravity?: boolean; gravity?: number; life?: number; extraUpdates?: number }) {
    super(x, y, vx, vy, damage, knockback, projId, { noGravity: true, life: opts?.life });
    const def = projectileData(projId);
    this.substeps = Math.max(1, (opts?.extraUpdates ?? def?.extraUpdates ?? 0) + 1);
    this.gravity = opts?.gravity ?? (opts?.noGravity === false ? 0.3 : 0);
    if (opts?.life !== undefined) this.life = opts.life;
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
    for (let s = 0; s < this.substeps; s++) {
      this.vy += this.gravity;
      this.x += this.vx;
      this.y += this.vy;
      const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
      const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
      if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; this.bounced = true; return; }
      for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
        const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
          hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks, pen?: number, crit?: boolean,
            pierce?: number, fromPlayer?: boolean) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
        if (e.dead || !projOverlap(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e)) continue;
        if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, 0, false, 0, true)) {
          playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
          game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
        }
        this.dead = true;   // penetrate 1 近似
        return;
      }
    }
  }
}

```

Math.PI : 0);\n    case 'upright':\n    case 'upright-mirror': return 0;\n    case 'grow': return age * (Math.PI / 300);\n    default: return 0;\n  }\n}\n\nexport class TownShot extends Entity {\n  w = 10; h = 10;\n  vx: number; vy: number;\n  damage: number;\n  knockback: number;\n  projId: number;\n  life = 600;\n  dead = false;\n  /** 弹附加 ai 参数(124/142/227 等特化弹;本仓多数弹不消费,仅存档语义) */\n  ai1 = 0; ai2 = 0;\n  /** 588 弹体 timeLeft=480(:54922-54925) */\n  private lifeOverride?: number;\n  /** 绘制 scale(SetDefaults;绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生 × scale,与判定盒解耦) */\n  drawScale = 1;\n  /** 出世 life(MountShot 不调 super.fixedUpdate,age 用 life 差值推导) */\n  private age0 = 600;\n  /** 翻滚/自旋累积角(tumble/up20 后段/spin 模式) */\n  rotAcc = 0;\n\n  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, knockback: number, projId: number,\n    opts?: { ai1?: number; ai2?: number; life?: number; noGravity?: boolean }) {\n    super();\n    this.x = x; this.y = y;\n    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;\n    this.damage = damage;\n    this.knockback = knockback;\n    this.projId = projId;\n    if (opts) {\n      if (opts.ai1 !== undefined) this.ai1 = opts.ai1;\n      if (opts.ai2 !== undefined) this.ai2 = opts.ai2;\n      if (opts.life !== undefined) this.lifeOverride = opts.life;\n      if (opts.noGravity) this.noGrav = true;\n    }\n    const def = projectileData(projId);\n    if (def) { this.w = def.width || 10; this.h = def.height || 10; }\n    this.drawScale = def?.scale ?? 1;\n    if (this.lifeOverride !== undefined) this.life = this.lifeOverride;\n    this.age0 = this.life;\n    // 589 五列变体:原版出生 ai[1]=rand5(NPC.cs:54953 末参),发射端未传时本地补\n    if (projId === 589 && (opts?.ai1 === undefined)) this.ai1 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);\n  }\n\n  private noGrav = false;\n\n  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const img = projSprite(this.projId);\n    const spec = townDrawSpec(this.projId);\n    const age = this.age0 - this.life;\n    const fIdx = townFrameIndex(this.projId, age, this.ai1, this.ai2);\n    // 帧图:横向条本地切(589/590),竖排行走 Arrow.projFrameImg,单帧整图\n    const cols = spec.cols;\n    const rows = cols > 0 ? 1 : projFrameCount(this.projId);\n    const drawImg: CanvasImageSource | null = cols > 0\n      ? stripCell(this.projId, cols, fIdx)\n      : (rows > 1 ? projFrameImg(this.projId, fIdx) : (img as HTMLImageElement | null));\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    const ang = townRotation(spec.rot, this.vx, this.vy, this.rotAcc, age, spec.spinRate);\n    ctx.rotate(ang);\n    // 水平镜像族:mirror-right(585 :54715)/upright-mirror(880 AI_183)\n    if ((spec.rot === 'mirror-right' || spec.rot === 'upright-mirror') && this.vx < 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);\n    if (drawImg && (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).complete !== false\n      && ((drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth > 0 || (drawImg as HTMLCanvasElement).width > 0)) {\n      // 原生帧尺寸 × SetDefaults scale(589 曾整条 100×28 压进 10×10、590 压成 14×2.9)\n      const s = this.drawScale;\n      const iw = (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalWidth || (drawImg as HTMLCanvasElement).width;\n      const ih = (drawImg as HTMLImageElement).naturalHeight || (drawImg as HTMLCanvasElement).height;\n      // 586 净化脉冲:scale = age/100 钳 1(AI_111 :38729-38733)\n      const gs = spec.rot === 'grow' ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0.02, age / 100)) : 1;\n      ctx.drawImage(drawImg, (-iw * s * gs) / 2, (-ih * s * gs) / 2, iw * s * gs, ih * s * gs);\n    } else {\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#D8C8A0';\n      ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }\n    if (!this.noGrav) this.vy += GRAV;\n    // 翻滚/自旋累积(aiStyle 2 :21517 / 自旋款)——up20 前 20t 由姿态锁覆盖\n    const spec = townDrawSpec(this.projId);\n    const age = this.age0 - this.life;\n    if (spec.rot === 'tumble' || (spec.rot === 'up20' && age > 20)) {\n      this.rotAcc += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);\n    } else if (spec.rot === 'spin') {\n      this.rotAcc += spec.spinRate;\n    }\n    this.x += this.vx;\n    this.y += this.vy;\n    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);\n    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);\n    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }\n    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;\n        hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks, pen?: number, crit?: boolean, pierce?: number, fromPlayer?: boolean) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };\n      if (e.dead || !projOverlap(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e)) continue;\n      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, 0, false, 0, false)) {\n        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);\n        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');\n      }\n      this.dead = true; // 穿透 1(城镇自卫弹均为 penetrate 1 或 -1 特化弹,近似单发)\n      return;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** 横向多列条单格缓存(id+列号 → canvas)。projFrameImg 只支持竖排行,589/590 在此切 */\nconst stripCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();\nfunction stripCell(projId: number, cols: number, idx: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {\n  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return null;\n  const img = projSprite(projId);\n  if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return null;\n  const i = Math.max(0, Math.min(cols - 1, idx));\n  const key = `${projId}|c${i}`;\n  let c = stripCache.get(key);\n  if (c) return c;\n  c = document.createElement('canvas');\n  c.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth / cols);\n  c.height = img.naturalHeight;\n  const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;\n  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n  cx.drawImage(img, Math.round(i * c.width), 0, c.width, c.height, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);\n  if (stripCache.size > 512) stripCache.clear();\n  stripCache.set(key, c);\n  return c;\n}\n"
}

Wait, there's a bug: in draw(), when cols > 0, `rows = 1` and `drawImg = stripCell(...)` — good. When cols === 0, rows = projFrameCount(id); if rows > 1 → projFrameImg. But projFrameImg returns null if the image isn't loaded. Then drawImg = null → fallback rect. But for single-frame (rows===1) I pass `img` directly (which may not be complete). The check `(drawImg as HTMLImageElement).complete !== false` handles that.

Hmm, but there's an issue: when rows > 1 and projFrameImg returns null (image not yet loaded), we lose the fallback to the raw img. In Arrow.ts, `frameImg ?? img` is used. Let me match that: `rows > 1 ? (projFrameImg(...) ?? img) : img`. But then for a multi-frame id with an unloaded image, drawImg = img (full strip) and the check catches incomplete. Fine.

Also — the old code checked `img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0` before drawing; mine checks complete !== false. For an HTMLImageElement that hasn't been assigned a src... projSprite always sets src. `complete` is false until loaded. OK.

Let me fix the drawImg line.
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T13:45:25.004Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
For 683: vanilla SetDefaults has `alpha = 255` (Projectile.cs:7003-7012) and AI_135 never reduces alpha → **the projectile is never drawn** (dust/gore only). The repo draws a visible box that scales with the expanding hitbox (up to 640×640) — doubly wrong.

Fix: remove the sprite draw entirely (vanilla = invisible). The effect is carried by the dust burst in fixedUpdate. I'll keep the draw method as a no-op with an explanatory comment.
```

</details>

 "old_string": "  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, _cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_961.png');\n    const s = this.scale();\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalAlpha = this.opacity;\n    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n    ctx.rotate(this.rot + Math.PI / 2);\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    if (img) ctx.drawImage(img, -16 * s, -16 * s, 32 * s, 32 * s);\n    else { ctx.fillStyle = '#9FD8F0'; ctx.fillRect(-16 * s, -16 * s, 32 * s, 32 * s); }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}",
 "new_string": "  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, _cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_961.png');\n    const s = this.scale();\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalAlpha = this.opacity;\n    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n    // 朝右贴图:原版 rotation = velocity.ToRotation()（:48485）,无 +π/2 偏置——\n    // 曾 +π/2 把横置冰泪竖过来（G11 旋转模式）。AI 的 scaleY 生长斜坡\n    // GetLerpValue(30,25,ai0)（Main.cs:31009-31011）在 ai0<=20 即 Kill 的寿命内恒 1,死码\n    ctx.rotate(this.rot);\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    if (img) {\n      // Frame(1,5)（Main.cs:31004）:贴图 200×240 → 帧格 200×48,× scale=Opacity*ai[1]（:48536）。\n      // 曾压成 32×32 判定盒（横向 6.25× / 纵向 5× 失真）。origin (16, 24) = (16, 帧高/2)\n      // （Main.cs:31005）——泪体自锚点向 velocity 方向前伸\n      const fh = img.naturalHeight / 5;\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, Math.round(this.frame * fh), img.naturalWidth, Math.round(fh),\n        -16 * s, -(fh / 2) * s, img.naturalWidth * s, fh * s);\n    } else { ctx.fillStyle = '#9FD8F0'; ctx.fillRect(-16 * s, -16 * s, 32 * s, 32 * s); }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}"
}

 "old_string": "export class DeerclopsRubbleProj extends Entity {\n  w = 32; h = 32;\n  dead = false;\n  life = 220;\n  constructor(x: number, y: number, public vx: number, public vy: number,\n    private dmg: number, private frame: number) {\n    super();\n    this.x = x - this.w / 2; this.y = this.y - this.h / 2;\n  }\n  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; game.onProjectileDeath?.(962, this.x, this.y); return; }\n    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.3, 16);                    // aiStyle 1 重力\n    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;                     // tileCollide=false 穿落\n    const p = game.player;\n    if (p && !p.dead\n      && p.x < this.x + this.w && p.x + p.w > this.x && p.y < this.y + this.h && p.y + p.h > this.y) {\n      game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy);\n    }\n  }\n  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, _cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_962.png');\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n    ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx));\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    if (img) {\n      // 12 变体 = 4 列×3 行（Main.projFrames[962]=3）;ai1 ∈ 6..11 → 行 floor(/4)\n      const fw = img.naturalWidth / 4, fh = img.naturalHeight / 3;\n      const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(11, this.frame));\n      ctx.drawImage(img, (f % 4) * fw, Math.floor(f / 4) * fh, fw, fh, -16, -16, 32, 32);\n    } else { ctx.fillStyle = '#887860'; ctx.fillRect(-16, -16, 32, 32); }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}",
 "new_string": "export class DeerclopsRubbleProj extends Entity {\n  w = 32; h = 32;\n  dead = false;\n  life = 220;\n  /** 出世姿态角（:54039 localAI[0]==0 时 rotation = velocity.ToRotation() 一次定型,\n   *  此后不随当前速度更新——曾每帧重算 atan2(vy,vx)） */\n  private readonly spawnRot: number;\n  constructor(x: number, y: number, public vx: number, public vy: number,\n    private dmg: number, private frame: number) {\n    super();\n    this.x = x - this.w / 2; this.y = y - this.h / 2;\n    this.spawnRot = Math.atan2(vy, vx);\n  }\n  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; game.onProjectileDeath?.(962, this.x, this.y);                                 // 原生帧格（scale=1,不再归一 32×32）\n      ctx.drawImage(img, Math.round((f % 3) * fw), Math.round(Math.floor(f / 3) * fh),\n        Math.round(fw), Math.round(fh), -cw / 2, -ch / 2, cw, ch);\n    } else { ctx.fillStyle = '#887860'; ctx.fillRect(-16, -16, 32, 32); }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}"

return; }\n    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.3, 16);                    // aiStyle 1 重力\n    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;                     // tileCollide=false 穿落\n    const p = game.player;\n    if (p && !p.dead\n      && p.x < this.x + this.w && p.x + p.w > this.x && p.y < this.y + this.h && p.y + p.h > this.y) {\n      game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy);\n    }\n  }\n  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer, _cam: import('../render/Camera').Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const img = r.atlas?.ensureVImage('vanilla/Projectile_962.png');\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n    ctx.rotate(this.spawnRot);\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    if (img) {\n      // 网格 = Frame(3,4)（Main.cs:32828-32834:projFrames[962]=3 横 × 4 纵,帧格 34×34）;\n      // frameX = frame%3 / frameY = frame/3。曾按 4 列×3 行（25.5×45.33 非整数格）切片\n      // 并归一到 32×32 判定盒（纵横比翻转,G11）\n      const fw = img.naturalWidth / 3, fh = img.naturalHeight / 4;\n      const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(11, this.frame));\n      ctx.drawImage(img, Math.round((f % 3) * fw), Math.round(Math.floor(f / 3) * fh),\n        Math.round(fw), Math.round(fh), -fw / 2, -fh / 2, fw, fh);\n    } else { ctx.fillStyle = '#887860'; ctx.fillRect(-16, -16, 32, 32); }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}"
}
```

Wait — there's a subtlety with the 965 origin + mirror. In vanilla, when spriteDirection == -1 (vx<0), `dir = FlipHorizontally` is applied AND origin.X = 20. The XNA flip mirrors the source rect around its center in the rotated frame, but the ORIGIN stays at (20, 42) in texture space (unmirrored coordinates). So the anchor point is texture x=20 for left-facing.

In canvas: translate → rotate(rotation) → scale(-1,1) → drawImage(img, -ox, -oy). With scale(-1,1) applied AFTER rotate, the local x axis is mirrored, so drawing at (-ox, -oy) places texture-x=ox at the anchor. For vx<0: ox=20 ✓. For vx>=0: no mirror, ox = W-20 = 60 ✓.

Hmm wait, but vanilla's origin for spriteDirection==1 is (Width-20) = 60. And when flipped (spriteDirection==-1), origin.X = 20. So yes my implementation matches: vx>=0 → 60, vx<0 → 20. ✓

Now — one more check on the 965 `rotation` field: the repo's InsanityShadowProj sets `this.rotation = atan2(vy,vx) + (vx<0 ? π : 0)` — and vanilla additionally applies the flip. ✓ consistent with my draw.

Harness:
```ts
interface Rec { calls: Array<{ m: string; a: unknown[] }> }
function makeCtx(): { rec: Rec; ctx: unknown } {
  const rec: Rec = { calls: [] };
  const ctx = new Proxy({}, {
    get: (_t, k) => {
      if (k === 'canvas') return undefined;
      return (...a: unknown[]) => { rec.calls.push({ m: String(k), a }); };
    },
  });
  return { rec, ctx };
}
```
Careful: properties set (globalAlpha = x, imageSmoothingEnabled = false) — Proxy `set` trap needed or they become no-ops on the target. With a Proxy over {}, setting a property just writes to the target. Fine. But `get` returns a function for EVERY property including ones that are read... draw methods don't read ctx properties. OK. }

      // sh = 112/4\n    expect(d.a[2]).toBeCloseTo(0, 5);        // sy = 帧 0\n    expect(d.a[7]).toBeCloseTo(28, 5);\n    expect(d.a[8]).toBeCloseTo(28, 5);\n  });\n\n  it('子弹 14:2×20 曳光 ×1.2 = 2.4×24(曾画 4×40)', () => {\n    setScale(14);\n    const shot = new TownShot(0, 0, 8, 0, 24, 3, 14);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(null);\n    shot.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[7]).toBeCloseTo(2.4, 5);\n    expect(d.a[8]).toBeCloseTo(24, 5);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ TownShot 旋转模式\ndescribe('TownShot per-type 旋转模式(原版 rotation 赋值锚)', () => {\n  const rotOf = (id: number, vx = 3, vy = -2, rotAcc = 0, age = 0) =>\n    townRotation(townDrawSpec(id).rot, vx, vy, rotAcc, age, townDrawSpec(id).spinRate);\n\n  it('默认族 +π/2(AI_001 尾 :54868):1/2/14/134/267/242/587/606', () => {\n    for (const id of [1, 2, 14, 134, 267, 242, 587, 606, 357]) {\n      expect(townDrawSpec(id).rot).toBe('up');\n      expect(rotOf(id)).toBeCloseTo(Math.atan2(-2, 3) + Math.PI / 2, 6);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('aiStyle 2 姿态锁族 48/520:前 20t +π/2,之后翻滚累积(:21970-21975/:21517)', () => {\n    for (const id of [48, 520]) expect(townDrawSpec(id).rot).toBe('up20');\n    expect(rotOf(48, 3, -2, 9.9, 10)).toBeCloseTo(Math.atan2(-2, 3) + Math.PI / 2, 6);\n    expect(rotOf(48, 3, -2, 9.9, 30)).toBe(9.9);          // 翻滚累积接管\n  });\n\n  it('aiStyle 2 翻滚族 21/162/583/589(:21517)与滚动族 30/588/930/721(:44913)', () => {\n    for (const id of [21, 162, 583, 589]) expect(townDrawSpec(id).rot).toBe('tumble');\n    for (const id of [30, 588, 930, 721]) {\n      expect(townDrawSpec(id).rot).toBe('roll');\n      expect(rotOf(id, 5, 0)).toBeCloseTo(0.5, 6);        // vx*0.1\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('恒 0 直立族(:133/135 aiStyle 16 支、15 aiStyle 8、24 aiStyle 14、590 AI_112、950 AI_186)', () => {\n    for (const id of [133, 135, 15, 24, 590, 950]) {\n      expect(townDrawSpec(id).rot === 'upright').toBe(true);\n      expect(rotOf(id, 3, -2)).toBe(0);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('582 自旋 π/10(:32893)/ 669 自旋 0.25(aiStyle 68 :29047)', () => {\n    expect(townDrawSpec(582).spinRate).toBeCloseTo(Math.PI / 10, 6);\n    expect(townDrawSpec(669).spinRate).toBeCloseTo(0.25, 6);\n  });\n\n  it('585 MIRROR 分支(:54715):ToRotation + 向左补 π,draw 侧向左水平镜像', () => {\n    expect(townDrawSpec(585).rot).toBe('mirror-right');\n    expect(rotOf(585, -3, -2)).toBeCloseTo(Math.atan2(-2, -3) + Math.PI, 6);\n    expect(rotOf(585, 3, -2)).toBeCloseTo(Math.atan2(-2, 3), 6);\n    // 行为侧:vx<0 → scale(-1,1) 被调用\n    setScale(585);\n    const left = new TownShot(0, 0, -6, 0, 16, 2, 585);\n    const lr = makeAtlasR(null);\n    left.draw(lr.r as never, undefined as never);\n    expect(lr.calls.some((c) => c.m === 'scale' && c.a[0] === -1 && c.a[1] === 1)).toBe(true);\n    const right = new TownShot(0, 0, 6, 0, 16, 2, 585);\n    const rr = makeAtlasR(null);\n    right.draw(rr.r as never, undefined as never);\n    expect(rr.calls.some((c) => c.m === 'scale')).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('880 AI_183(:40418-40430):rotation 恒 0 + 向左镜像', () => {\n    expect(townDrawSpec(880).rot).toBe('upright-mirror');\n    expect(rotOf(880, -3, -2)).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it('586 AI_111_DryadsWard(:38728-38733):rotation = age×π/300,scale 生长钳 1', () => {\n    expect(townDrawSpec(586).rot).toBe('grow');\n    expect(rotOf(586, 0, 0, 0, 50)).toBeCloseTo(50 * (Math.PI / 300), 6);\n    setScale(586);\n    const shot = new TownShot(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 3, 586, { noGravity: true });\n    shot.life = shot.life - 50;                        // age=50 → gs=0.5\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(null);\n    shot.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[7]).toBeCloseTo(24 * 0.5, 5);           // 24×80 × 0.5\n    expect(d.a[8]).toBeCloseTo(80 * 0.5, 5);\n  });\n\n  it('翻滚/自旋累积在 fixedUpdate 推进(tumble/spin)', () => {\n    const shot = new TownShot(0, 0, 4, 0, 10, 1, 21);  // tumble\n    const hooks = { world: { store: { isSolid: () => false } }, enemies: () => [] } as never;\n    shot.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    shot.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(shot.rotAcc).toBeCloseTo(2 * (4 * 0.03), 6);\n    const spin = new TownShot(0, 0, 4, 0, 10, 1, 582); // spin π/10\n    spin.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    spin.fixedUpdate(1, hooks);\n    expect(spin.rotAcc).toBeCloseTo(2 * (Math.PI / 10), 6);\n  });\n\n  it('未知弹型回落 AI_001 默认 +π/2', () => {\n    expect(townDrawSpec(9999).rot).toBe('up');\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ TownShot 帧号\ndescribe('townFrameIndex(帧源锚)', () => {\n  it('880 每 tick +1 钳末帧(AI_183 :40427-40430,8 帧 → 钳 7)', () => {\n    expect(townFrameIndex(880, 3, 0, 0)).toBe(3);\n    expect(townFrameIndex(880, 100, 0, 0)).toBe(7);\n  });\n  it('589 列 = ai[1](出生 rand5,NPC.cs:54953)', () => {\n    expect(townFrameIndex(589, 0, 4, 0)).toBe(4);\n    expect(townFrameIndex(589, 0, 9, 0)).toBe(4);      // 钳上界\n    expect(townFrameIndex(589, 0, -1, 0)).toBe(0);     // 钳下界\n  });\n  it('590 基组 = ai[2](0→0..2 / 1→3..5)+ age/4 三帧循环(AI_112 :33025-33038)', () => {\n    expect(townFrameIndex(590, 0, 0, 0)).toBe(0);\n    expect(townFrameIndex(590, 4, 0, 0)).toBe(1);\n    expect(townFrameIndex(590, 9, 0, 0)).toBe(0 + 2);\n    expect(townFrameIndex(590, 0, 0, 1)).toBe(3);\n    expect(townFrameIndex(590, 5, 0, 1)).toBe(3 + 1);\n  });\n  it('其余单帧/恒帧 0(585 族 AI_001 不推帧)', () => {\n    expect(townFrameIndex(585, 999, 0, 0)).toBe(0);\n    expect(townFrameIndex(1, 999, 0, 0)).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ SquidCloud 813\ndescribe('SquidCloud 813(AI_108 :32619-32767 三段视觉)', () => {\n  it('纯函数:生长 51-90 / 定格 91-120 / 收缩 >120', () => {\n    const v51 = squidCloudVisual(51);\n    expect(v51.scale).toBeCloseTo(1 / 40, 6);\n    expect(v51.rotation).toBeCloseTo(-Math.PI / 20, 6);\n    const v90 = squidCloudVisual(90);\n    expect(v90.scale).toBe(1);\n    expect(v90.rotation).toBeCloseTo(-40 * (Math.PI / 20), 6);       // -2π\n    const v120 = squidCloudVisual(120);\n    expect(v120.scale).toBe(1);\n    expect(v120.rotation).toBeCloseTo(-40 * (Math.PI / 20) - 30 * (Math.PI / 60), 6);\n    const v140 = squidCloudVisual(140);\n    expect(v140.scale).toBeCloseTo(1 - 20 / 60, 6);\n  });\n\n  it('绘制 = 原生 72×72 × scale(不再归一 32×32 判定盒)', () => {\n    const c = new SquidCloud(100, 100);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 100; t++) c.fixedUpdate(1, { spawnParticles: () => {} } as never);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(72, 72));\n    c.draw(r);\n    const d = calls.find((x) => x.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[3]).toBeCloseTo(72, 5);                 // dh = 72(scale=1 段)\n    expect(d.a[2]).toBeCloseTo(72, 5);                 // dw\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ 月噬弹 456\ndescribe('MoonLeechProj 456(Main.projFrames[456]=4,Main.cs:8490;aiStyle 85 不推帧)', () => {\n  it('整条 22×96 切成帧 0 格 22×24,不再压进 16×16(胶片条)', () => {\n    NEXT_IMG = { w: 22, h: 96 };\n    const src = { id: 1, cx: 0, cy: 0 } as never;\n    const player = { cx: 100, cy: 0 } as never;\n    const p = new MoonLeechProj(src, player);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(null);\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[4]).toBeCloseTo(24, 5);                 // sh = 96/4\n    expect(d.a[7]).toBeCloseTo(22, 5);                 // dw = 原生帧宽\n    expect(d.a[8]).toBeCloseTo(24, 5);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ DD2 681/683\ndescribe('DD2GoblinBomb 681(原生 14×20,Main.cs:29805/29811/29856)', () => {\n  it('目标矩形 = 原生 14×20(不再掐成 14×14),origin (7,13)', () => {\n    const b = new DD2GoblinBomb(0, 0, -5, 0, 20);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(14, 20));\n    b.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[0]).toBeCloseTo(-7, 5);                 // dx = -originX\n    expect(d.a[1]).toBeCloseTo(-13, 5);                // dy = -(h/2+num143=6)\n    expect(d.a[2]).toBeCloseTo(14, 5);                 // dw 原生\n    expect(d.a[3]).toBeCloseTo(20, 5);\n  });\n  it('velocity.X>0 → 水平镜像(:29811 ^= FlipHorizontally)', () => {\n    const right = new DD2GoblinBomb(0, 0, 5, 0, 20);\n    const rr = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(14, 20));\n    right.draw(rr.r as never, undefined as never);\n    expect(rr.calls.some((c) => c.m === 'scale' && c.a[0] === -1)).toBe(true);\n    const left = new DD2GoblinBomb(0, 0, -5, 0, 20);\n    const lr = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(14, 20));\n    left.draw(lr.r as never, undefined as never);\n    expect(lr.calls.some((c) => c.m === 'scale')).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('DD2OgreSmash 683(SetDefaults alpha=255,:7003-7012 → 原版不可见)', () => {\n  it('不绘制本体(曾画成随盒扩张的贴图,盒扩到 640²)', () => {\n    const s = new DD2OgreSmash(0, 0, 30);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(16, 16));\n    s.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    expect(calls.filter((c) => c.m === 'drawImage' || c.m === 'fillRect')).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ 鹿角怪 961/962/965\ndescribe('DeerclopsSpikeProj 961(Frame(1,5) Main.cs:31004;rotation=ToRotation :48485)', () => {\n  it('200×240 切五行 → 帧格 200×48 × scale(Opacity*ai1),不再 32×32', () => {\n    const p = new DeerclopsSpikeProj(0, 0, -Math.PI / 2, 13, 0.5);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 10; t++) p.fixedUpdate(1, { spawnParticles: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {}, playSfx: () => {} } as never);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(200, 240));\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[4]).toBeCloseTo(48, 5);                       // sh = 240/5\n    expect(d.a[7]).toBeCloseTo(200 * 1 * 0.5, 4);            // dw = 200 × scale\n    expect(d.a[8]).toBeCloseTo(48 * 1 * 0.5, 4);             // dh = 48 × scale\n  });\n  it('旋转 = 出生角本身(无 +π/2 偏置,曾把横置冰泪竖过来)', () => {\n    const p = new DeerclopsSpikeProj(0, 0, -Math.PI / 2, 13, 1);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(200, 240));\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const rot = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'rotate')!;\n    expect(rot.a[0]).toBeCloseTo(-Math.PI / 2, 6);\n  });\n  it('origin (16, 24) = (16, 帧高/2)(Main.cs:31005)——dx = -16×scale', () => {\n    const p = new DeerclopsSpikeProj(0, 0, 0, 13, 1);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(200, 240));\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[5]).toBeCloseTo(-16, 5);\n    expect(d.a[6]).toBeCloseTo(-24, 5);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('DeerclopsRubbleProj 962(Frame(3,4) Main.cs:32828-32834;:54039 出世定型)', () => {\n  it('102×136 按 3×4 网格切(帧格 34×34),不再 4 列×3 行非整数格', () => {\n    const p = new DeerclopsRubbleProj(0, 0, 3, -4, 18, 7);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(102, 136));\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    // frame=7 → frameX=7%3=1 → sx=34;frameY=2 → sy=68\n    expect(d.a[1]).toBeCloseTo(34, 5);\n    expect(d.a[2]).toBeCloseTo(68, 5);\n    expect(d.a[3]).toBeCloseTo(34, 5);\n    expect(d.a[4]).toBeCloseTo(34, 5);\n    expect(d.a[7]).toBeCloseTo(34, 5);                 // 原生帧格(scale=1,不归一 32×32)\n    expect(d.a[8]).toBeCloseTo(34, 5);\n  });\n  it('姿态角出生定型(:54039),不随当前速度重算', () => {\n    const p = new DeerclopsRubbleProj(0, 0, 3, -4, 18, 0);\n    p.vy = 16;                                          // 重力坠落中,姿态不变\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(102, 136));\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const rot = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'rotate')!;\n    expect(rot.a[0]).toBeCloseTo(Math.atan2(-4, 3), 6);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('InsanityShadowProj 965(原生 80×84;origin Main.cs:29934-29937;镜像 AI_187 :39850)', () => {\n  it('朝右:80×84 原生(origin X=W-20=60),不再 40×40', () => {\n    const p = new InsanityShadowProj({ cx: 0, cy: 0, vx: 0 }, 10, 0);\n    (p as unknown as { vx: number }).vx = 4;            // 朝右\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(80, 84));\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[0]).toBeCloseTo(-60, 5);\n    expect(d.a[1]).toBeCloseTo(-42, 5);\n    expect(d.a[2]).toBeCloseTo(80, 5);\n    expect(d.a[3]).toBeCloseTo(84, 5);\n  });\n  it('朝左:水平镜像 + origin X=20(spriteDirection=-1)', () => {\n    const p = new InsanityShadowProj({ cx: 0, cy: 0, vx: 0 }, 10, 0);\n    (p as unknown as { vx: number }).vx = -4;\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(fakeImg(80, 84));\n    p.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    expect(calls.some((c) => c.m === 'scale' && c.a[0] === -1 && c.a[1] === 1)).toBe(true);\n    const d = calls.find((c) => c.m === 'drawImage')!;\n    expect(d.a[0]).toBeCloseTo(-20, 5);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ LunarOrb 539/574\ndescribe('LunarOrb 539/574(原生帧 × scale,Main.cs:32812 列表族 :32889 origin Size/2)', () => {\n  beforeAll(() => {\n    // projFrameImg 走 document.createElement —— 桩出极简 canvas\n    vi.stubGlobal('document', {\n      createElement: () => {\n        const c = { width: 0, height: 0 };\n        const inner = makeRecCtx();\n        return Object.assign(c, { getContext: () => inner.ctx });\n      },\n    });\n  });\n  it('539:24×136 四帧行 → 帧格 24×34(不再归一 18×30 判定盒)', () => {\n    NEXT_IMG = { w: 24, h: 136 };\n    const orb = new LunarOrb(null, 539, 3, 0, 10, 0, 0);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(null);\n    orb.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.filter((c) => c.m === 'drawImage').at(-1)!;\n    expect(d.a[3]).toBeCloseTo(34, 5);                 // dh = 136/4\n    expect(d.a[2]).toBeCloseTo(24, 5);                 // dw = 原生帧宽\n  });\n  it('574:24×48 两帧行 → 帧格 24×24(不再归一 18×18)', () => {\n    NEXT_IMG = { w: 24, h: 48 };\n    const orb = new LunarOrb(null, 574, 3, 0, 10, 0, 0);\n    const { calls, r } = makeAtlasR(null);\n    orb.draw(r as never, undefined as never);\n    const d = calls.filter((c) => c.m === 'drawImage').at(-1)!;\n    expect(d.a[3]).toBeCloseTo(24, 5);                 // 48/2\n    expect(d.a[2]).toBeCloseTo(24, 5);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============================================================ 数据一致性闸门\ndescribe('帧数权威 = Main.projFrames(json 提取)对本批弹型', () => {\n  it('456=4 / 539=4 / 574=2 / 585=4 / 880=8(Main.cs:8490/:8498/:8500/:8637)', () => {\n    expect(projFrameCount(456)).toBe(4);\n    expect(projFrameCount(539)).toBe(4);\n    expect(projFrameCount(574)).toBe(2);\n    expect(projFrameCount(585)).toBe(4);\n    expect(projFrameCount(880)).toBe(8);\n  });\n  it('962=3(projFrames)但网格是 Frame(3,4)——竖排 3 与横向 4 不冲突(Main.cs:8645/:32828)', () => {\n    expect(projFrameCount(962)).toBe(3);\n  });\n});\n\n// 类型自锁:TownRotMode 覆盖九种(防误删分支)\ndescribe('TownRotMode 全枚举在表内出现(防分支误删)', () => {\n  const ALL: TownRotMode[] = ['up', 'up20', 'tumble', 'roll', 'spin', 'mirror-right', 'upright', 'upright-mirror', 'grow'];\n  for (const m of ALL) {\n    it(`模式 ${m} 存在于规格表`, () => {\n      const modeOf: Record<TownRotMode, number> = {\n        up: 1, up20: 48, tumble: 21, roll: 30, spin: 582,\n        'mirror-right': 585, upright: 15, 'upright-mirror': 880, grow: 586,\n      };\n      expect(townDrawSpec(modeOf[m]).rot).toBe(m);\n    });\n  }\n});\n"
}

Class fields initialize at instantiation. So each `new Image()` reads the CURRENT NEXT_IMG. That's fine.

OH — I see it. `setScale` sets NEXT_IMG = {w, h} — a plain object. The FakeImage reads `NEXT_IMG.w`. Fine. So where does Infinity come from? 

`cw = img.naturalWidth / cols` where cols = spec.cols = 0 → cw = naturalWidth/0 = Infinity! Bug in my DRAW code: `const cw = img.naturalWidth / cols` — when cols === 0, division by zero → Infinity. I need `cols > 0 ? naturalWidth/cols : naturalWidth`. Let me fix the draw code — good catch by the test!

In TownShot.draw:
```ts
const cols = spec.cols;
const rows = cols > 0 ? 1 : projFrameCount(this.projId);
const cw = img.naturalWidth / cols, ch = img.naturalHeight / rows;
```
cw = w/0 = Infinity when cols=0. ch = h/rows fine. So for all cols=0 ids, dw = Infinity → drawn infinitely wide?! That's a REAL bug in my new code (would draw garbage/huge). Must fix:

```ts
const cw = cols > 0 ? img.naturalWidth / cols : img.naturalWidth;
const ch = cols > 0 ? img.naturalHeight : img.naturalHeight / rows;
```

      // 网格 = Frame(3,4)（Main.cs:32828-32834:projFrames[962]=3 横 × 4 纵,帧格 34×34）;
